### [1] [VideoKR: Towards Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05259)
*Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yang Wang,Tingyu Song,Arman Cohan,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoKR 是一个面向知识密集与推理密集视频理解的大规模训练语料，配套评测集 VideoKR-Eval；基于该数据进行后训练可显著提升模型在专家域视频推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解训练数据难以充分覆盖需要外部知识、复杂推理和真实视频证据整合的任务，容易依赖文本捷径，限制了模型在知识密集型视频推理中的能力提升。

Method: 作者构建了包含 31.5 万个视频推理样例、覆盖 14.5 万个新收集 CC 授权专家领域视频的 VideoKR；采用 human-in-the-loop、面向技能的样例生成流程，逐步增强推理深度，并控制问题与 CoT 理由的难度、多样性和可靠性；同时构建专家标注评测集 VideoKR-Eval，并在 SFT 到 GRPO 的标准后训练流程中验证效果。

Result: 实验表明，使用 VideoKR 后训练的模型在知识密集型视频推理任务上优于既有后训练方法，同时在通用视频推理任务上保持竞争力；消融实验进一步分离并验证了 VideoKR 各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作说明高质量、技能导向且可靠的数据设计是推进视频推理能力的重要因素，VideoKR 为未来知识密集型视频理解模型训练与评测提供了有价值的数据资源和方法参考。

Abstract: We introduce VideoKR, the first large-scale training corpus specifically designed to strengthen knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video understanding. It comprises 315K video reasoning examples over 145K newly collected, CC-licensed, expert-domain videos. We develop a human-in-the-loop, skill-oriented example generation pipeline that targets progressively deeper video reasoning capabilities while ensuring the difficulty, diversity, and reliability of both the examples and their CoT rationales. We also curate VideoKR-Eval, a new expert-annotated benchmark where questions require genuine video understanding and knowledge-intensive reasoning rather than textual shortcuts. Our experiments show that, under a standard SFT$\rightarrow$GRPO pipeline, models post-trained on VideoKR outperform prior post-training approaches on knowledge-intensive video reasoning while remaining competitive on general video reasoning, highlighting data design as a key driver of progress in video reasoning. We further conduct comprehensive ablations to isolate the contributions of VideoKR, providing actionable insights for future work.

</details>


### [2] [NIV: Neural Axis Variations for Variable Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05261)
*Nadav Benedek,Ariel Shamir,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NIV 是一种将静态字体自动转换为可变字体的神经方法，直接基于矢量字形轮廓预测多设计轴下的点位移，并生成标准可用的可变字体文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变字体构建依赖字体设计专家手工定义字形在字重、字宽、倾斜、光学尺寸等设计轴上的变化，流程耗时且劳动密集；论文旨在自动化这一过程，降低静态字体扩展为可变字体的成本。

Method: 方法 NIV 输入字形轮廓和目标设计轴，直接在矢量几何上预测每个轮廓点的位移；提出 Property Embedding 机制以建模多个设计轴之间的交互，从而在统一框架中实现一致的多轴连续变化。模型使用从 Google Fonts 可变字体构建的新数据集训练，包含超过一百万个 variation tuples。

Result: 模型可泛化到未见过的字符、未见过的字体风格、高复杂度 CJK 字形以及分布外手写输入；生成结果为标准 variable font 文件，可由现有渲染引擎进行连续插值。作者还开源了数据集、训练与推理代码以及训练好的模型。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，神经形变模型能够有效合成具有连续参数变化的结构化几何对象；在字体设计中，NIV 为静态字体自动生成多轴可变字体提供了实用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Variable fonts enable continuous variation of glyph geometry along semantic design axes such as weight, width, slant, and optical size. However, constructing a variable font from a static font remains a labor-intensive process requiring expert typographic design and manual specification of glyph variation data. We introduce NIV (Neural Axis Variations), a method that automatically converts a static font into a fully functional variable font. Given glyph outlines and a set of desired design axes, NIV predicts per-point displacements. The model operates directly on vector glyph geometry and employs a novel Property Embedding mechanism that captures interactions between multiple axes, enabling consistent multi-axis variation within a unified framework. We train NIV on a newly constructed dataset derived from variable Google Fonts, comprising over one million variation tuples. The resulting model generalizes across unseen code points, unseen font styles, high-complexity CJK glyphs, and even out-of-distribution handwriting inputs. The generated outputs are standard variable font files supporting continuous interpolation via existing rendering engines. To facilitate research, we release the dataset, the complete training and inference implementation, and trained models at https://github.com/ndvbd/NIV. Beyond typography, our approach demonstrates how structured geometric objects with continuous parametric variation can be synthesized using neural deformations.

</details>


### [3] [Personal AI Agent for Camera Roll VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05275)
*Thao Nguyen,Krishna Kumar Singh,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee,Yuheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出个人相机胶卷视觉问答任务，并构建 camroll 数据集与 camroll-agent，用于在大规模个人照片流中进行高效检索和推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人相机胶卷包含多年、数百到数千张高度个性化照片，现有长上下文智能体多面向文本记忆，难以稳定处理视觉细节、用户特定语境与长期一致性需求。

Method: 作者收集并人工标注模拟真实使用场景的问题，形成包含 50 名用户、31,476 张图像和 2,500 个问答对的 camroll 数据集；同时设计具备层次化记忆和少量导航工具的会话式 camroll-agent，以在大规模个性化视觉记忆中定位相关信息。

Result: 实验显示，camroll-agent 在该任务上优于多种基线和长上下文理解智能体方法。

Conclusion: camroll 数据集和 camroll-agent 表明，个性化视觉记忆对长上下文推理提出了不同于标准文本记忆的挑战，需要针对视觉细节、一致性和用户特定上下文设计专门方法。

Abstract: We study the personal camera roll visual question answering setting. In this setting, a conversational AI assistant can access a user's personal camera roll and retrieve relevant photos to answer queries, ranging from simple factual questions (e.g., ``Name of the food I tried yesterday?'') to more open-ended ones (e.g., ``Recommend some dishes I have never eaten before''). Given the vast nature of the personal camera roll (i.e., multiple years, hundreds to thousands of photos), a successful AI assistant needs to understand a long-horizon, highly personalized visual content stream in order to navigate and locate the correct and/or relevant information. To support this, we collect and manually annotate questions that mimic real-world usage. The final dataset, camroll, contains 50 users, 31,476 images, and 2,500 QA pairs. We further design camroll-agent, a conversational AI agent equipped with hierarchical memory and a minimal set of tools for efficient navigation over large, personalized visual memory. Experimental results show that camroll-agent outperforms numerous baselines and methods for long-context understanding AI agents system. Together, the camroll dataset and camroll-agent highlight the gap in AI agents' long-context reasoning: personalized visual memory requires different approaches from standard long-context textual memory, especially when consistency, visual details, and user-specific context are present.

</details>


### [4] [Do Models Share Safety Representations? Cross-Model Steering for Safe Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05290)
*Tobia Poppi,Silvia Cappelletti,Sara Sarto,Florian Schiffers,Garin Kessler,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模型安全 steering 框架：在源模型中学习安全潜在方向，通过仅使用良性数据拟合的轻量对齐迁移到目标生成模型，并在推理时应用，以减少不安全输出且尽量保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型安全控制方法通常依赖特定模型，需要针对每个新架构重新训练或定制干预；作者希望验证安全行为是否能表示为可迁移的潜在方向，从而在不同生成器之间复用。

Method: 从源 LLM 的安全/不安全成对提示中估计安全方向；用目标侧仅含良性数据的轻量对齐模块将该方向映射到目标生成器；推理时施加该方向进行安全控制。进一步提出多向量扩展，以捕获类别特定的安全行为，实现更细粒度控制。

Result: 在文本到图像和文本到视频任务、多个源-目标模型组合上，迁移得到的安全方向在降低 ASR 与保持 CLIP-Score/FID 之间取得的权衡，接近使用目标侧不安全数据原生学习方向的方法；同时不需要访问目标侧不安全数据。

Conclusion: 安全相关行为并非完全局限于单一模型内部，而可能以跨模型共享的表示几何形式存在；可迁移潜在方向为轻量、模块化、可复用且不依赖目标侧不安全数据的安全机制提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent progress in generative modeling has made safety control a central challenge, yet existing approaches remain largely model-specific, requiring retraining or tailored interventions for each new architecture. In this work, we ask whether safety can be represented as a portable latent direction, learned once and reused across heterogeneous generators. We introduce the first framework for cross-model safety steering, in which a safety direction is estimated in a source LLM from paired safe-unsafe prompts, transported to a target generator through a lightweight alignment fitted on benign data alone, and applied at inference time. Crucially, our pipeline never accesses unsafe data on the target side, isolating whether safety can be transferred through shared representation geometry. Beyond a single global direction, we also identify a multi-vector extension that captures category-specific safety behaviors, enabling more selective control. We evaluate our approach in text-to-image and text-to-video generation across diverse source-target model pairs. Across models, transferred safety directions achieve ASR reduction and CLIP-Score/FID trade-offs comparable to directions learned natively on the target model using unsafe data, while requiring no target-side unsafe data. This indicates that safety improvements do not come at the expense of generation quality. Our results point to a modular view of safety: safety-relevant behavior is not purely model-local, but can be controlled through latent directions that persist across models. This suggests a new path toward lightweight, reusable safety mechanisms that do not require target-side unsafe data.

</details>


### [5] [TopoPult-SSL: Gland-Mask-Free Cross-Device Meibomian Gland Segmentation via Self-Distilled Weak Clinical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05347)
*Nicolò Savioli,Luca Del Tongo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TopoPult-SSL，用于跨设备睑板腺分割：先在无目标域腺体密集标注条件下用眼睑轮廓与临床元数据进行弱先验适配，再在有标注时通过教师蒸馏得到单次推理的紧凑学生模型；在 MGD-1k→CAMG 基准上取得优于对比方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新临床成像设备会造成显著域偏移，而目标设备上的密集睑板腺掩膜标注成本高；相比之下，眼睑轮廓、Pult 分级和形态学比例等临床信号更容易获得。因此需要一种能利用廉价弱监督信息进行跨设备适配，并在有少量/完整目标掩膜时进一步提升性能的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架 TopoPult-SSL。第一阶段在不将目标域腺体掩膜用于训练损失的情况下，对源域训练模型进行目标域适配，依赖由目标眼睑掩膜和临床元数据驱动的四类弱先验锚点。第二阶段在目标域腺体掩膜可用时，将多个互补的第一阶段教师模型通过监督式自蒸馏压缩为单个紧凑学生模型，实现单次前向推理。

Result: 在公开 MGD-1k 到 CAMG 跨设备基准上进行验证。蒸馏模型达到 Dice 0.716±0.006，最佳 0.726，超过 UA-MT 的 0.710，并略低/接近但以单模型单次推理超过集成教师的实用性表现（集成教师 0.720）。无腺体掩膜的第一阶段变体 Precision 达 0.694，显著优于 SAM/MedSAM 的 0.30–0.34，统计显著性 p<0.001。

Conclusion: TopoPult-SSL 有效缓解跨设备睑板腺分割的域偏移问题，能在缺乏目标域密集腺体标注时利用廉价临床先验实现可部署性能，并在有标注时通过蒸馏获得高效、准确的单模型方案。

Abstract: Every new clinical imaging device creates a domain shift where dense gland masks are expensive yet cheap clinical signals -- eyelid outlines, Pult grades, morphometric ratios -- are routinely recorded. We present TopoPult-SSL, a two-stage framework for cross-device meibomian gland segmentation. Stage 1 adapts a source-trained model without target gland masks in the training loss, using four weak-prior anchors driven by target eyelid masks and clinical metadata only. Stage 2, when target gland masks are available, distils complementary Stage-1 teachers into a single compact student via supervised self-distillation. We develop and validate the technique on the public MGD-1k to CAMG research benchmark (1,000 to 100 images, different device), where the distilled model achieves Dice 0.716+/-0.006 (best 0.726), surpassing UA-MT (0.710) and the ensemble teacher (0.720) -- with a single pass. The gland-mask-free Stage-1 variant reaches Precision 0.694 vs. 0.30-0.34 for SAM/MedSAM (p<0.001), enabling deployment without dense gland contouring. Code and reproducibility scripts are released.

</details>


### [6] [LightVesselNet: An Ultra-Lightweight Sub-100K Parameter Network for Retinal Blood Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05354)
*Shadman Sobhan,Farhana Jalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightVesselNet 是一种仅含 75K 参数的轻量级视网膜血管分割网络，在多个公开数据集上以较低计算成本实现了具有竞争力的分割性能，适合边缘设备和低资源临床场景部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习视网膜血管分割模型虽然精度较高，但通常参数量大、计算开销高，难以部署到移动筛查工具或资源受限的边缘设备。因此，论文旨在设计一种兼顾准确性、效率和泛化能力的轻量级分割模型。

Method: 提出 LightVesselNet，采用紧凑型编码器—解码器结构；引入通道注意力与空间注意力以增强有效特征表达；在瓶颈层使用多尺度特征聚合模块以捕获不同尺度血管信息；解码器采用子像素上采样策略提升重建效率；通过专门的边缘残差连接在解码过程中保留细小血管边界与结构细节。

Result: 模型仅有 75K 参数，但在 DRIVE、STARE、CHASEDB1、FIVES 和 HRF 五个公开数据集上取得较好表现：敏感性分别为 0.8189、0.8499、0.8640、0.8634、0.8096；Dice 系数分别为 0.8070、0.8072、0.8181、0.8649、0.7686。相较部分先进模型，在性能—参数量或性能—计算量权衡方面更具优势；跨数据集实验也显示出一定泛化能力。

Conclusion: LightVesselNet 在极低参数量下实现了较好的视网膜血管分割性能和效率，适合资源受限的临床筛查、移动设备和边缘计算应用，是一个具有实际部署潜力的轻量化医学图像分割模型。

Abstract: Retinal blood vessel segmentation plays a vital role in the early detection of diabetic retinopathy and glaucoma. While recent deep learning models have achieved great segmentation accuracy, they typically require heavy computational resources, making real-world deployment on edge devices difficult. In this paper, we propose LightVesselNet, an efficient neural network designed for retinal vessel segmentation in a resource-constrained environment. Despite containing only 75K parameters, LightVesselNet performs competitively with much larger models. The network employs a compact encoder decoder architecture enhanced with channel and spatial attention mechanisms, a multi-scale feature aggregation module at the bottleneck, and a subpixel upsampling strategy in the decoder. A dedicated edge residual connection preserves fine vessel detail throughout decoding. Extensive experiments on five publicly available datasets: DRIVE, STARE, CHASEDB1, FIVES, and HRF, yield sensitivity scores of 0.8189, 0.8499, 0.8640, 0.8634, 0.8096, and Dice coefficients of 0.8070, 0.8072, 0.8181, 0.8649, and 0.7686, respectively. LightVesselNet shows improved efficiency (Performance vs Parameter or GFlops) compared to State-of-the-Art models. Cross-dataset evaluation confirms the model's generalisation capability. Overall, LightVesselNet is a strong candidate for deployment in low-resource clinical settings and mobile screening tools.

</details>


### [7] [Recovering Physically Plausible Human-Object Interactions from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05359)
*Dingbang Huang,Etienne Vouga,Qixing Huang,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePHO 从单目视频中重建物理合理的人-物交互，通过物理仿真与强化学习优化初始运动估计，显著减少穿插、漂浮等不合理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动学的方法虽然能生成视觉上合理的人-物交互动作，但常出现物理不合理伪影，例如人体与物体相互穿插、物体漂浮、接触不稳定等。因此需要一种能保证物理一致性的重建方法。

Method: 方法首先获得单目视频的人-物交互运动学初始估计，然后在物理模拟器中训练强化学习策略，使其复现该交互过程。为应对初始估计中的噪声，作者提出带有双重自更新机制的自适应采样策略，用于选择信息量高且可靠的关键帧，逐步提升策略训练与重建质量。

Result: 在两个标准 HOI 基准数据集上，RePHO 相比现有最先进方法在物理合理性指标上取得明显提升，生成的人-物交互序列更加稳定、接触更自然，并减少了穿插和漂浮问题。

Conclusion: RePHO 表明，将运动学估计、物理仿真和强化学习结合，可以有效提升单目视频中人-物交互重建的物理一致性，尤其适用于存在噪声初始估计的复杂交互场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RePHO, a method to reconstruct physically plausible human-object interactions (HOI) from monocular videos. While existing kinematic-based approaches produce visually plausible motion, they often result in physically implausible artifacts such as interpenetration and object floating. To overcome these issues, we introduce a physics-guided reconstruction framework. We begin with a kinematic estimate and then refine it by training a policy with reinforcement learning (RL). This policy is optimized to reproduce the interaction in a physics simulator. Because kinematic estimates are typically noisy, naive RL training can fail. Therefore, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy with a dual self-updating mechanism that can identify the frames with the most informative and reliable kinematic reconstruction. Our process progressively improves reconstruction quality and yields physically consistent HOI sequences. We demonstrate our approach on two standard HOI benchmarks and achieve clear improvements in physical plausibility metrics over state-of-the-art methods. Project Page: https://dingbang777.github.io/RePHO/

</details>


### [8] [Biomazon: A Multimodal Dataset for 3D Forest Structure and Biomass Modeling in the Amazon Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05368)
*Sayan Mandal,Rocco Sedona,Simon Besnard,Mikhail Urbazaev,Morris Riedel,Ehsan Zandi,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Biomazon：一个覆盖亚马逊盆地、20 m 分辨率的多模态基准数据集，用于联合预测 GEDI 相对高度（RH）垂直剖面与地上生物量密度（AGBD），并提供标准化划分、评估协议和基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习流程多将冠层高度代理指标（如 RH95/RH98）或 AGBD 作为独立标量目标预测，难以建模森林垂直结构的有序剖面，也缺少可直接用于机器学习的多模态基准来评估 RH 百分位之间的物理一致性。

Method: 构建 Biomazon 数据集，将 GEDI RH 与 AGBD 目标同 Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2、Copernicus DEM、Dynamic World LULC 和 AlphaEarth embeddings 等多源预测变量配对；采用标准化空间划分和评估协议；以共享编码器-解码器和任务特定预测头为基线，系统开展模型骨干/规模、模态贡献、辅助嵌入在独立与融合设置下作用的消融实验，并比较单目标与联合目标训练。

Result: 论文报告了统一训练协议下的单目标与联合目标基线表现，量化了不同模型规模、输入模态和辅助嵌入带来的性能差异及权衡；同时与时间尺度匹配、区域对齐的已有网格产品（如 GEDI L4D RH10-RH98 与 AGBD）进行对比，以 contextualize 基线性能。

Conclusion: Biomazon 及其协议和基线结果为热带森林结构一致的 RH 剖面预测与结构-生物量建模提供了参考基准，有助于推动物理一致、多模态、联合建模方法的发展。

Abstract: Accurate, spatially explicit characterization of tropical forest structure is essential for carbon accounting and ecosystem monitoring, yet most ML pipelines predict canopy-top height proxies (e.g., RH95/RH98) or AGBD as separate scalar targets, rather than learning the forest vertical structure as an ordered profile. The community lacks a ML-ready multimodal benchmark for predicting the entire GEDI RH profile jointly with AGBD, or for evaluating methods that enforce physically consistent ordering across RH percentiles. We address this with Biomazon, a 20 m multimodal benchmark dataset over the Amazon Basin that pairs GEDI RH and AGBD targets with multi-sensor predictors (Sentinel-1/2, ALOS-2 PALSAR-2, Copernicus DEM, Dynamic World LULC, and AlphaEarth embeddings) under standardized spatial splits and evaluation protocols. Using a shared encoder-decoder with task-specific heads as a baseline framework, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study of (i) backbone/model scale, (ii) modality contributions, and (iii) the use of auxiliary embeddings under standalone and fusion settings, and we report both single-target and joint-target results to quantify tradeoffs under a unified training protocol. Finally, we contextualize baseline performance through regionally aligned comparisons against existing gridded products, including GEDI L4D RH10-RH98 and AGBD, at matching temporal scale. Biomazon, together with the accompanying protocols and baseline results, establishes a reference benchmark for future work on structurally consistent RH-profile prediction and structure-biomass modeling in tropical forests.

</details>


### [9] [Three-Dimensional Retinal Microvasculature Restoration in OCT Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05375)
*Yukun Guo,Min Gao,Tristan T. Hormel,Steven T. Bailey,Thomas S. Hwang,Yali Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的OCTA三维体数据血管结构恢复方法，可从单次OCTA扫描中提升毛细血管成像质量与微血管保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCTA可用于视网膜微血管成像，但单次采集易受伪影影响，导致血流定量和无灌注区域评估不可靠。既有方法多关注二维投影或横断面图像的去噪、投影伪影去除和信号增强，未充分利用血管的三维解剖结构。

Method: 作者设计了一个深度学习网络：以EfficientNet-B5为编码器，解码器结合空间与通道并行压缩激励模块，并通过跳跃连接保持空间分辨率。模型输入三个相邻B-frame，预测恢复后的中间B-frame；训练/评估的参考真值由多次扫描平均生成。

Result: 相较原始单次OCTA体数据，模型在PSNR和SSIM上显著提升：PSNR从22.23±0.78提升至26.16±1.26，SSIM从0.72±0.03提升至0.91±0.02，均p<0.001。Dice重叠系数显示微血管保真度在2D和3D不同血管层中均显著改善，分别至少提升3.8%和51.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够从单个OCTA体数据中恢复更接近真实解剖结构的毛细血管网络，显著改善图像质量和三维微血管保真度，有助于更可靠的视网膜血流和无灌注区域定量分析。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomographic angiography (OCTA) is a powerful technique for imaging retinal microvasculature. However, acquiring reliable quantification of retinal blood flow and areas of retinal nonperfusion is challenging because of imaging artifacts. Existing methods primarily focus on noise suppression, projection artifact removal, or signal enhancement to improve the image quality of OCTA in cross-sectional or two-dimensional (2D) en face projections, while neglecting the intrinsic three-dimensional vascular architecture. In this study, we propose a deep learning-based algorithm for restoring capillary anatomical vasculature from a single OCTA volume. The network consists of an EfficientNet-B5 encoder and a decoder incorporating concurrent spatial and channel squeeze-and-excitation modules, connected via skip connections to preserve spatial resolution. Three adjacent B-frames are used as input to predict the restored middle B-frame. We evaluated the performance of the model using the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index measure (SSIM) against ground truth generated from averaging multiple scans. The results show that the proposed model significantly (both p < 0.001) improved image quality compared with the original single OCTA volume, with a PSNR of 26.16 +/- 1.26 vs. 22.23 +/- 0.78 and an SSIM of 0.91 +/- 0.02 vs. 0.72 +/- 0.03. The proposed model also significantly (p < 0.001) improved microvascular fidelity, measured by the Dice coefficient overlap between the model output and ground truth, in both 2D and 3D by at least 3.8% and 51.2%, respectively, across several different vascular slabs.

</details>


### [10] [Deep Learning-assisted AMD Staging based on OCT and OCT Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05379)
*Yukun Guo,Tristan T. Hormel,An-Lun Wu,Liqin Gao,Min Gao,Steven T. Bailey,Yali Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估基于OCT/OCTA的深度学习模型，用于自动分级年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）严重程度；其中基于病理生物标志物图的模型整体表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMD分级对临床诊断、随访和治疗决策具有重要意义，但人工评估依赖专家经验且耗时。研究希望利用OCT与OCTA影像中的结构和血流信息，建立自动化、客观且可重复的AMD严重程度分级方法。

Method: 纳入271名50岁及以上、不同AMD严重程度的参与者，共分析351只眼的2030个OCT/OCTA体数据。依据AREDS简化严重程度量表将AMD分为无AMD、早期、中期和晚期四级。研究构建三类EfficientNet架构深度学习模型：基于分割病理特征生成的生物标志物图、基于二维en face OCT/OCTA投影、基于三维OCT/OCTA体数据；采用输入归一化、数据增强和五折交叉验证进行训练与评估。

Result: 所有模型均显示较强分级能力，与参考标准具有较高一致性（QWK≥0.83）。生物标志物模型整体性能最高（QWK=0.85±0.03），并在早期AMD检测上表现最佳（F1=0.59±0.14）。三维模型与二维OCT/OCTA模型表现相近（QWK均约0.83），二维模型具有最高精确率（0.79±0.06），且最能准确识别无AMD眼。

Conclusion: 基于OCT/OCTA的深度学习模型能够较准确地自动完成AMD严重程度分级。相比直接使用二维或三维影像输入，基于病理生物标志物的模型表现更均衡，尤其在早期AMD识别方面具有潜在临床价值。

Abstract: To develop and evaluate deep learning models for automated grading of age-related macular degeneration (AMD) severity using optical coherence tomography (OCT) and OCT angiography (OCTA) data. Two hundred seventy-one participants aged >= 50 years with varying AMD severities. Central macular 6 x 6 mm OCT/OCTA volumes were acquired using a swept-source OCTA system (SOLIX; Visionix/Optovue Inc., CA). AMD severity was graded into four stages (No AMD, Early AMD, Intermediate AMD, and Advanced AMD) according to the AREDS simplified severity scale. Three deep learning models were developed using different input modalities: (1) biomarker maps derived from segmented pathological features, including retinal fluid, drusen, geographic atrophy (GA), and macular neovascularization (MNV); (2) two-dimensional (2D) en face OCT and OCTA projections; and (3) three-dimensional (3D) OCT/OCTA volumes. EfficientNet-based architectures were trained using normalized inputs, data augmentation, and five-fold cross-validation. A total of 2,030 OCT/OCTA volumes from 351 eyes of 271 participants were analyzed. All models demonstrated strong AMD staging performance with substantial agreement with the reference standard (QWK >= 0.83). The biomarker-based model achieved the highest overall performance (QWK = 0.85 +/- 0.03, mean +/- standard deviation) and the best detection of early AMD (F1-score = 0.59 +/- 0.14). The 3D model achieved performance comparable to the 2D OCT/OCTA model (QWK = 0.83 +/- 0.04 vs. 0.83 +/- 0.09), while the 2D OCT/OCTA model showed the highest precision (0.79 +/- 0.06) and most accurately identified eyes without AMD. Deep learning models using OCT/OCTA data can accurately and automatically grade AMD severity. Among the evaluated approaches, the biomarker-based model provided the most balanced performance and showed particular value for early AMD detection.

</details>


### [11] [UniPixie: Unified and Probabilistic 3D Physics Learning via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05399)
*Qilin Huang,Quynh Anh Huynh,Long Le,Chen Wang,Chuhao Chen,Ryan Lucas,Eric Eaton,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UNIPIXIE 将单图像物理属性预测从单一点估计重构为可控的连续材料属性分布学习，可生成多种物理合理且可仿真的材料场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈网络通常只能从视觉外观预测一组固定物理属性，无法表达真实世界中同一外观可能对应多种材料刚度与动力学行为的内在不确定性；同时，已有方法在不同物理求解器之间的参数可迁移性不足。

Method: 提出 UNIPIXIE 框架，在 PIXIEMULTIVERSE 数据集上学习从单个视觉输入到材料属性“最软到最硬”连续参数化路径的直接映射；通过一个直观控制参数生成不同物理合理的材料场，并采用统一架构输出适配多类物理求解器的仿真参数，包括 MPM、基于 LBS 的降阶形变模型和 Spring-Mass 系统。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够生成丰富多样且合理的动力学效果，并相较最强确定性基线将杨氏模量预测误差降低超过 50%。

Conclusion: UNIPIXIE 有效弥合了静态点估计与现实物理属性连续不确定性之间的差距，同时提升了材料属性预测的可控性、多样性和跨物理求解器可用性。

Abstract: Existing feed-forward networks excel at predicting a single set of physical properties from visual appearance, but this point-estimate paradigm fundamentally fails to capture the real world's inherent physical ambiguity. We address this by reframing physics prediction as a task of learning a controllable, continuous distribution of material properties. We introduce UNIPIXIE, a framework trained to predict a continuous and parameterized path of physically plausible material properties from a single visual input. By learning a direct mapping along an object's softest-to-stiffest spectrum on our PIXIEMULTIVERSE dataset, UNIPIXIE allows for controllable generation of diverse, physically valid material fields via a single intuitive parameter. Crucially, UNIPIXIE introduces a novel unified architecture to produce simulation-ready parameters for diverse physics solvers, including continuum-based Material Point Method (MPM), reduced-order deformation based on Linear Blend Skinning (LBS), and anchor-based Spring-Mass systems, addressing a key portability issue in prior work. Experiments show our approach not only generates a rich variety of plausible dynamics but also reduces Young's Modulus prediction error by over 50% against the strongest deterministic baseline, bridging the gap between static point estimates and the continuous nature of physical reality. Project page: https://unipixie.github.io/

</details>


### [12] [Would you still call this Dax? Novel Visual References in VLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05409)
*Ada Defne Tür,Gaurav Kamath,Joyce Chai,Siva Reddy,Benno Krojer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NVRD 数据集，用于评估视觉语言模型在上下文中学习全新视觉概念、尤其是与预训练先验冲突的概念时的能力，并发现模型相较人类存在明显过泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少探讨 VLM 在接触新视觉参照后如何将其映射到语言，尤其当新参照与模型预训练知识相矛盾时；同时，既有工作多关注熟悉概念的视觉增强，难以模拟人类遇到真正新概念的情境。

Method: 作者构建 Novel Visual References Dataset（NVRD），包含 90 个视觉概念、19,176 张图像，每个原始对象最多配有 20 个逐步扰动版本以测试泛化能力。实验评估 3 个开源和 2 个闭源 VLM，并收集 2,400 个人类判断进行人机对比。

Result: 结果显示：模型在上下文中学习与先验知识冲突的新概念时表现困难；模型与人类对视觉扰动的敏感性存在相关性，但模型会显著过泛化，把新学到的标签扩展到人类会拒绝的刺激上。

Conclusion: NVRD 为研究人类和机器的视觉概念学习提供了新的语料与基准；当前 VLM 虽能部分捕捉视觉扰动趋势，但在新概念获取和泛化边界控制方面仍明显落后于人类。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), like human learners, are frequently exposed to new visual concepts, but how they map novel visual references to language after exposure remains largely underexplored, particularly when those references contradict prior knowledge from pre-training. To study this, we present the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD): 19,176 images spanning 90 visual concepts across different levels of visual novelty, each with up to 20 increasingly perturbed versions of the original object to probe generalization. Unlike prior work on visual augmentations of familiar concepts, NVRD comprises entirely novel, open-ended stimuli constructed from scratch, mirroring how humans encounter genuinely new concepts. We evaluate 3 open- and 2 closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments for direct human-model comparison, and find that (i) models struggle to acquire novel concepts in-context when they contradict prior knowledge, and (ii) while models and humans show correlated sensitivity to visual perturbations, models significantly overgeneralize, extending learned labels to stimuli that humans reject. We contribute NVRD as a corpus and benchmark for research on visual concept learning in both humans and machines.

</details>


### [13] [Disentangled Fine-Grained Prototype Learning for Incomplete Image-Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05455)
*Feixiang Zhou,Jianyang Xie,Zhuangzhi Gao,Qinkai Yu,Fu Wang,Yuheng Fan,Jing Li,Zheheng Jiang,Yitian Zhao,Yanda Meng,He Zhao,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DFPL通过细粒度原型学习缓解图像-表格多模态学习中的模态缺失问题，在多个基准上优于既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像与表格属性在语义粒度和数据分布上高度异质，现有基于全局平均特征的模态不变表示学习只能捕获粗粒度一致性，难以处理细粒度语义与分布错位，限制了缺失模态场景下互补信息的利用。

Method: 提出DFPL框架：使用SSPM提取紧凑且多样的共享原型与模态特异原型，并进行原型级解耦以减少模态内冗余相关；设计PFA在统一原型空间中同时进行原型级分布匹配与原型到类别的语义对齐；引入CMA从全局层级和原型层级自适应聚合共享语义与模态特异特征，用于鲁棒预测。

Result: 在三个图像-表格多模态基准数据集上进行广泛实验，结果显示DFPL在多种模态缺失设置下均优于以往方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向图像-表格异质模态的细粒度原型建模、对齐与聚合能够更有效地保持跨模态语义和分布一致性，从而提升缺失模态条件下的预测鲁棒性。

Abstract: The missing-modality problem poses a significant challenge in image-tabular multimodal learning across a wide range of multimedia applications, including product understanding, recommendation systems, and medical diagnosis. This challenge is particularly pronounced when the two modalities are highly heterogeneous, as images and tabular attributes differ substantially in their semantic granularity and data distributions. Existing methods learn modality-invariant representations through disentanglement and alignment over global token-averaged features, capturing only coarse cross-modal consistency and overlooking fine-grained semantic and distributional misalignment, which hampers the exploitation of complementary cues under missing modalities. To address this, we propose DFPL, a novel framework for fine-grained prototype learning. Specifically, Shared-Specific Prototype Modeling (SSPM) extracts compact and diverse shared and modality-specific prototypes, and further performs prototype-level disentanglement to suppress redundant intra-modality correlations. Additionally, we propose a Prototype-guided Fine-grained Alignment (PFA) module that jointly enforces prototype-level distribution matching and prototype-to-class semantic alignment within a unified prototype space, thereby preserving both fine-grained distributional and semantic consistency across modalities. We further introduce a Class-aware Multi-scale Aggregation (CMA) module to adaptively aggregate shared semantics and modality-specific characteristics from global and prototype levels for robust predictions. Extensive experiments on three diverse image-tabular benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our method compared to the previous approaches under various missing-modality settings. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [14] [Horse Eye Blink Detection and Classification for Equine Affective State Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05458)
*João Alves,Signe Møller-Skuldbøl,Pia Haubro Andersen,Rikke Gade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究马匹视频中眨眼动作的自动检测，用于辅助疼痛与情绪/应激状态评估；比较了YOLOv12、光流阈值和微调VideoMAE三种方法，并取得较高F1表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马匹半眨眼和全眨眼是疼痛与压力的重要面部动作单元指标，但属于细微微表情，人工观察容易遗漏，逐帧视频检查成本高，因此需要可靠的自动化视频检测方法来支持马匹福利监测。

Method: 作者在公开马匹视频数据集上开发并评估三类眨眼分类方法：基于帧的YOLOv12检测器、基于光流幅值阈值的方法，以及微调后的VideoMAE视频模型，用于眨眼分类和二分类眨眼检测任务。

Result: 实验在眨眼分类任务上达到macro-F1 0.898，在二分类眨眼检测任务上达到F1 0.926，显示出自动检测马匹细粒度面部动作单元的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，视频深度学习与运动分析方法可有效支持马匹眨眼自动识别，但细微动作单元检测仍具有固有挑战，未来需进一步提升鲁棒性、泛化能力和实际场景适用性。

Abstract: Automated detection of equine facial action units (AUs) is a promising yet under-explored avenue for pain and affective state assessment in horses. Half and full-blink movements are recognised indicators of pain and stress, but as micro-expressions, their subtle, fine-grained nature makes them easily missed by the naked eye and only discernible through frame-by-frame video inspection, making reliable automated detection from video a particularly demanding task. We develop and evaluate three methods for automated blink classification from horse videos: a frame-based YOLOv12 detector, an optical flow magnitude thresholding approach, and a fine-tuned VideoMAE model, tested on a publicly available dataset. We achieve a macro-F1 score of 0.898 when doing blink classification and 0.926 on binary blink detection. Our results highlight both the potential and the inherent challenges of fine-grained AU detection for equine welfare monitoring.

</details>


### [15] [ORACLE-CT: Anatomy-Aware Support Pooling for CT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05460)
*Lavsen Dahal,Yubraj Bhandari,Geoffrey Rubin,Joseph Y. Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORACLE-CT 是一种面向腹部 CT 疾病分类的解剖感知聚合框架，通过多器官分割限定与标签相关的解剖区域，在多个编码器和外部数据集上提升 AUROC/AUPRC，并增强可解释性与外部鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹部 CT 是大规模 3D 体数据，疾病证据通常局限于特定器官或解剖区域；而传统研究级分类器多采用全局平均池化或解剖无关注意力，导致局部诊断证据与全局特征聚合之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出 ORACLE-CT，一个编码器无关的解剖感知聚合框架。该方法利用多器官分割结果为不同疾病标签定义解剖支持区域，并将注意力/池化限制在相关区域内；支持单器官、多器官并集、比较式、局部化和全局等多种支持策略。实验结合 DINOv3、I3D–ResNet-121 和 Pillar–0 三类编码器，在 MERLIN 上端到端训练，并在 Duke–Abdomen 和 AMOS 上进行冻结外部迁移评估。

Result: 相较全局平均池化，支持区域掩膜池化在 MERLIN 上提升 DINOv3 的 macro-AUROC/AUPRC 从 0.838/0.638 至 0.858/0.676，提升 I3D–ResNet-121 从 0.829/0.617 至 0.848/0.659。在 10 标签外部评估中，DINOv3 在 Duke–Abdomen 从 0.802/0.628 提升至 0.835/0.683，在 AMOS 从 0.742/0.313 提升至 0.762/0.350；I3D–ResNet-121 也获得类似收益。Pillar–0 的主要收益来自学习式注意力，解剖掩膜带来的额外提升较小。

Conclusion: ORACLE-CT 通过把疾病预测与相关解剖区域对齐，提高了腹部 CT 疾病分类的判别性能和外部泛化能力，同时保留了预测与解剖证据之间可审计、可解释的联系。

Abstract: Abdominal CT disease classification is challenging because each scan is a large 3D volume with many possible findings, while diagnostic evidence is often confined to specific organs or anatomical compartments. Most study-level classifiers aggregate encoder features using anatomy-agnostic pooling or attention, creating a mismatch between localized disease evidence and global evidence aggregation. We propose ORACLE--CT, an encoder-agnostic anatomy-aware aggregation framework that uses multi-organ segmentation to define label-specific anatomical supports and restrict attention pooling to relevant regions. The framework supports single-organ, multi-organ union, comparative, localized, and global support strategies.
  We evaluate ORACLE--CT with three encoder families: DINOv3, I3D--ResNet-121, and the radiology-native Pillar--0 encoder. Models are trained end-to-end on MERLIN and evaluated internally and under frozen external transfer to Duke--Abdomen and AMOS. Compared with global average pooling, support-masked pooling improved MERLIN macro-AUROC/AUPRC from 0.838/0.638 to 0.858/0.676 for DINOv3 and from 0.829/0.617 to 0.848/0.659 for I3D--ResNet-121. On harmonized 10-label external evaluation, DINOv3 improved on Duke--Abdomen from 0.802/0.628 to 0.835/0.683 and on AMOS from 0.742/0.313 to 0.762/0.350, with similar gains for I3D--ResNet-121. For Pillar--0, most gains came from learned attention, with smaller additional benefit from anatomical masking. ORACLE--CT improves discrimination and external robustness while preserving an auditable link between predictions and anatomical evidence.

</details>


### [16] [Formal Concept Lattices are Good Semantic Scaffolds for Concept-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05471)
*Deepika SN Vemuri,Sayanta Adhikari,Ankit Saha,Krishn Vishwas Kher,Vineeth N Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用形式概念分析中的形式概念格作为语义层级脚手架，引导深度网络在不同深度学习不同抽象层级的概念，从而提升可解释性、干预效果与概念表征的层级一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念型模型通常把所有语义概念视为扁平集合，并在单一网络层学习，忽略了人类语义理解中“从一般到具体”的层级结构；同时，深度网络虽天然学习视觉特征层级，但该层级往往未与显式语义层级对齐。

Method: 借鉴形式概念分析，构建形式概念格来表示概念之间的层级关系，并利用概念在格中的泛化/特化程度决定其应在网络的哪个深度层被学习，使网络形成分阶段、语义有依据的表征。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够产生更可解释的嵌入表示，支持更有效的概念干预，并学习到既有语义意义又具有层级结构的概念表征。

Conclusion: 形式概念格可作为一种原则性语义结构，将显式概念层级与神经网络深度表征对齐，从而改进概念型模型的可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: Learning semantics is essential for deep learning models to be interpretable and better aligned with human reasoning. Concept-based models approach this by representing classes through meaningful semantic abstractions, but typically treat all concepts as a flat, unstructured set learned at a single neural network layer. This overlooks a fundamental property of human semantic understanding: concepts being organized hierarchically, from general to specific. While deep networks do learn a hierarchy of visual features, this structure is rarely aligned with explicit semantic hierarchies. Drawing on Formal Concept Analysis, we demonstrate that formal concept lattices provide principled semantic scaffolds to guide neural network learning. These lattices naturally identify where in the network concepts should be learned based on their level of generality. This allows the model to develop staged, semantically grounded representations throughout its depth. Empirical results on real-world datasets show that our models produce more interpretable embeddings, support more effective interventions, and learn concept representations that are both meaningful and hierarchically structured.

</details>


### [17] [Can We Predict The Human Preference For Text-to-Image Content Prior To Generation And Is It Even Useful To Do So?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05478)
*Joong Ho Kim,Keith G. Mills*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究扩散模型中随机噪声种子对生成质量的影响，探索在实际生成前预测人类偏好指标得分，并利用该预测以低硬件开销提升生成图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成过程具有随机性，初始噪声会显著影响输出质量；尤其在本地部署的小模型中，这种影响更明显。传统视觉指标不足以反映人类偏好，因此需要研究能否在生成前预测人类偏好分数，从而避免浪费计算资源并提升结果质量。

Method: 作者围绕人类偏好指标（HPM）展开实验：首先评估在图像生成前基于随机种子/初始噪声预测标量HPM得分的可行性；随后利用预测结果选择更优生成条件或种子，以改善最终图像质量；同时比较不同HPM在该任务中的适用性。

Result: 研究发现，生成前预测HPM得分是可行的，并且可被用于提升生成图像质量；该方法能够在几乎可忽略的硬件开销下实现收益。

Conclusion: 随机噪声种子并非不可控因素，其对扩散模型输出质量的影响可以被提前建模和利用。通过预测人类偏好指标并据此筛选生成过程，可在低成本条件下改善文本到图像生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion Models (DM) have revolutionized text-driven generation by enabling the synthesis of high-quality, photorealistic visual content from user prompts. Whereas prior advances in visual generation such as VAEs and GANs were primarily evaluated on perceptual or visual similarity metrics such as FID PSNR, DM advances have fostered the development of more advanced Human Preference Metrics (HPM) that model and quantify human judgment as scalar values. However, DMs synthesize content using an inherently stochastic process where random noise seeds generation. The initial random noise directly affects the quality of generated outputs, both qualitatively and quantitatively. This influence is pronounced in smaller models for local deployment scenarios. Given this phenomenon, we first investigate to what extent we can predict scalar HPM scores prior to committing compute resources for generation. Further, we then investigate to what extent we can leverage such prediction to improve the quality of generated images, and also study which HPMs are best suited for this task. Our investigation reveals that not only is this possible, but that it is feasible to achieve negligible hardware overhead.

</details>


### [18] [LLM-Guided ANN Index Optimization for Human-Object Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05489)
*Shahrzad Esmat,Chaunte W. Lacewell,Sameh Gobriel,Nilesh Jain,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种阶段感知的LLM代理，用于多阶段检索系统的超参数优化，在强耦合参数空间中优于传统HPO方法，并具备跨VDBMS迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多阶段检索系统包含高度耦合的配置参数，而TPE、基于高斯过程的贝叶斯优化等传统HPO方法通常依赖参数独立性假设，难以有效探索此类耦合配置空间，限制了检索效率与质量的联合优化。

Method: 设计阶段感知的大语言模型代理，使其基于完整优化历史生成下一轮配置，并将搜索过程划分为探索、利用和微调阶段；在质量约束吞吐指标SIEVE下，对向量数据库检索系统进行优化，并在不同数据集和VDBMS平台上验证。

Result: 在HICO-DET基准与Intel VDMS上，该方法相较Optuna TPE提升33.3%，相较VDTuner提升34.2%，相较UniIR获得15.3倍吞吐提升；在GLDv2上各方法差距约1%以内，在SIFT1M上约3.6%以内；在Milvus上无需修改即可在三个数据集上排名第一。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的阶段感知优化器更适合处理强参数耦合的多阶段检索系统，其优势随参数耦合程度增加而增强，并展现出良好的跨数据集与跨VDBMS平台迁移性。

Abstract: Retrieval systems underpin modern AI applications -- spanning visual search, recommendation engines, and multi-modal question answering. Modern multi-stage retrieval systems require the joint optimization of highly coupled parameters, yet traditional hyperparameter optimization (HPO) methods -- including Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE) and Gaussian Process Bayesian Optimization -- rely on an independence assumption that fundamentally prevents them from navigating these coupled configuration spaces. We address this limitation with a phase-aware large language model (LLM) agent that conditions each proposal on its full optimization history, navigating the coupled parameter space across phase-partitioned exploration, exploitation, and fine-tuning stages. Evaluated on the HICO-DET human-object interaction retrieval benchmark using Intel VDMS (Visual Data Management System), our agent outperforms Optuna TPE by +33.3% and VDTuner by +34.2% under SIEVE (Safeguarded Index Evaluation of Vector-search Efficiency, a quality-constrained throughput metric), delivering a 15.3x throughput gain over UniIR. Validation across three benchmarks confirms that the agent's advantage grows with the degree of parameter coupling: +33.3% on HICO-DET (high coupling), methods converge within 1% on GLDv2 (moderate coupling) and within 3.6% on SIFT1M (near-independent control). Cross-system validation on Milvus confirms the optimizer ranks first on all three datasets without modification, demonstrating transferability across vector database management system (VDBMS) platforms.

</details>


### [19] [Unpaired RGB-Thermal Gaussian-Splatting Using Visual Geometric Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05491)
*Jean Cordonnier,Chenghao Xu,Olga Fink,Malcolm Mielle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需成对标定的RGB-热成像多模态新视角合成框架，实现跨模态位姿对齐与联合3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-热成像NVS方法通常依赖精确标定的成对图像或双目系统，限制了方法的可扩展性与实际部署；同时，已有重建方法可能产生各模态内部效果较好但跨模态不一致的重建结果。

Method: 使用VGGT分别对RGB与热成像数据估计相机位姿；通过跨模态特征匹配器结合Procrustes算法对两组位姿进行对齐；在对齐基础上提出多模态3D Gaussian Splatting，直接从非配对RGB与热图像中学习；并设计基准评估框架同时衡量单模态合成质量与多模态一致性。

Result: 在多样场景实验中，该方法在热成像新视角合成上取得具有竞争力的性能，同时保持RGB合成质量；实验还表明现有方法可能存在跨模态一致性不足的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作降低了RGB-热成像多模态NVS对成对标定数据的依赖，提升了实际适用性，并强调跨模态一致性应作为多模态重建的重要评估维度。

Abstract: Multi-modal novel view synthesis (NVS) combining RGB and thermal imagery enables precise 3D scene reconstruction with visual and thermal information. However, existing methods typically rely on precisely calibrated RGB-thermal image pairs or stereo setups, limiting scalability and practical deployment. To address this, we introduce a framework for unpaired RGB-thermal NVS that leverages VGGT, a 3D feed-forward transformer architecture, to independently estimate camera poses for each modality. The pose sets are then aligned using the Procrustes algorithm with a cross-modal feature matcher, enabling joint registration without paired calibration. Building on this alignment, we further propose a multi-modal 3D Gaussian Splatting approach that learns directly from unpaired RGB and thermal images. Experiments on diverse scenes demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance in thermal view synthesis while maintaining RGB fidelity. Moreover, we show that existing reconstruction approaches can produce modality-specific reconstructions that lack cross-modal consistency. We thus introduce a benchmarking framework to rigorously evaluate both per-modality image synthesis and the multi-modal coherence of reconstructed scenes.

</details>


### [20] [Robust Scene Transfer for PointGoal Navigation via Privileged Sensor Guided Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05506)
*Amirhossein Zhalehmehrabi,Tiziano Tezze,Alberto Castelini,Alessandro Farinelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种用于 PointGoal 导航的传感器引导自适应对比学习框架，在训练阶段利用特权 LiDAR 信息指导视觉表征学习，部署时仅使用单目 RGB 和任务相关输入，实现更强的跨场景迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航策略在跨环境迁移时容易依赖外观或场景特定捷径，导致在外观、语义或环境分布变化下性能下降。作者希望利用训练阶段可用但部署阶段不可用的几何传感信息，引导视觉编码器学习更具导航相关性的结构表征。

Method: 训练阶段使用 LiDAR 作为特权感知信号，构建几何感知相似度度量与自适应温度缩放机制，改进对比学习目标，使视觉嵌入关注导航相关几何结构而非场景外观。视觉编码器先独立预训练，随后冻结并作为强化学习策略的感知骨干。此外，通过在表征预训练与策略学习之间引入跨阶段域不匹配，抑制环境特定捷径。

Result: 在高保真仿真中的大量实验表明，该方法显著提升了室内和室外多样环境下的策略级场景迁移能力。部署时无需 LiDAR，仅依赖单目 RGB、目标位置和本体感知信号。该方法在严重外观和语义变化下优于大型预训练视觉模型和标准对比学习基线。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，训练阶段的特权几何传感信息可以有效指导视觉表征学习，并在不增加部署传感器成本的情况下提升 PointGoal 导航的泛化与迁移性能。发布的多模态数据集也可促进后续特权引导视觉导航研究。

Abstract: We propose a sensor-guided adaptive contrastive learning framework for visual representation learning in PointGoal navigation. During training, privileged LiDAR sensing guides the contrastive objective through a geometry-aware similarity metric and adaptive temperature scaling, encouraging visual embeddings to capture navigation-relevant structure rather than scene-specific appearance. The resulting encoder is pretrained independently, frozen, and used as the perceptual backbone for reinforcement learning, decoupling representation learning from policy optimization. We further introduce a cross-stage domain mismatch between representation pretraining and policy learning to suppress environment-specific shortcuts and promote reliance on task-relevant features.
  Extensive experiments in high-fidelity simulation demonstrate that our approach significantly improves policy-level scene transfer across diverse indoor and outdoor environments. At deployment, the agent relies only on monocular RGB observations together with standard task-related inputs such as goal position and proprioceptive signals, without access to LiDAR or other privileged sensors. Our method outperforms large pretrained vision models and standard contrastive baselines under severe appearance and semantic shifts. We also release a multimodal dataset to support future research on privileged-guided visual representation learning for navigation. The code is available at:

</details>


### [21] [BRepCLIP: Contrastive Multimodal Pretraining on BRep Primitives for CAD Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05515)
*Muhammad Usama,Didier Stricker,Mohammad Sadil Khan,Muhammad Zeshan Afzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BRepCLIP 是一个将 CAD 原生边界表示（BRep）与文本、图像嵌入对齐的对比预训练框架，显著提升了 CAD 检索、零样本分类和生成评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 表征学习主要集中在点云和网格，而 CAD 的原生格式 BRep 能精确描述参数曲面、曲线及拓扑结构，却很少被用于表征学习。论文旨在利用 BRep 的结构和几何优势，构建更适合 CAD 理解的多模态表示。

Method: 方法将每个 CAD 对象建模为由面 token 和边 token 组成的序列，并为曲面几何和曲线几何使用独立离散词表；同时加入空间描述符和语义描述符，以编码曲面类型与曲线基元。随后使用 Transformer 编码器聚合为全局 BRep 嵌入，并通过联合对比学习目标与 CLIP 的文本编码器和图像编码器进行对齐。

Result: BRepCLIP 相比点云类方法生成了更具判别性和语义基础的嵌入。在 ABC、CADParser 和 Automate 数据集上，相比 OpenShape 的 Top-1 检索分别提升 40.4%、22.0% 和 23.9%；在 FabWave 零样本分类任务上 Top-1 提升 15%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，面向 CAD 原生结构的 BRep 预训练能够显著提升多模态 CAD 理解能力，并可作为 CAD 感知的相似度度量，用于文本或图像条件 CAD 生成结果的评估。

Abstract: Learning representations of CAD models is a largely open problem. While 3D representation learning has flourished around point clouds and meshes, the native format of CAD - boundary representations BReps, which encodes exact parametric surfaces, curves, and their topology, has received little attention as a representation learning substrate. We introduce BRepCLIP, the first framework to align BRep geometry with language and image embeddings through contrastive pretraining. We model each CAD object as a sequence of face and edge tokens with separate discrete vocabularies for surface and curve geometry, augmented with spatial and semantic descriptors that capture surface types (e.g., cylindrical, torus, NURBS) and curve primitives (e.g., line, arc, B-spline). A transformer encoder aggregates these tokens into a global BRep embedding, aligned with CLIP's text and image encoders via a joint contrastive objective. BRepCLIP generates more discriminative and semantically grounded embeddings than existing point-based alternatives, improving Top-1 retrieval over OpenShape by 40.4%, 22.0%, and 23.9% on ABC, CADParser, and Automate, respectively, and improving zero-shot classification on FabWave by 15% in Top-1 score. We further demonstrate its utility as a CAD-aware similarity metric for evaluating text and image-conditioned CAD generation, establishing the importance of structure-aware pretraining for multimodal CAD understanding. Project page is available at https://muhammadusama100.github.io/BrepClip2026/

</details>


### [22] [Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench: A Bilingual Multimodal Benchmark for Cognitively Informed Evaluation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05531)
*Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Omid Ghahroodi,Anas Madkoor,Marzia Nouri,Doratossadat Dastgheib,Mohamed Hefeeda,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BloomBench 是一个基于布鲁姆认知分类法的英阿双语多模态基准，用于系统诊断视觉语言模型在六类认知层级上的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测多聚焦零散任务，难以揭示模型真实推理能力、具体认知短板以及跨语言多模态能力差距，因此需要更系统、认知 grounded 且具包容性的评测框架。

Method: 作者提出 BloomBench，围绕 Remember、Understand、Apply、Analyze、Evaluate、Create 六个认知层级设计图像-问题-答案任务；采用半自动构建流程，并通过分层混合质量保证协议进行验证，以保证规模化、语言一致性和文化适配性。

Result: 实验显示，先进 VLM 在语义理解方面表现较强，但在事实回忆和创造性综合方面明显不足；同时，模型在阿拉伯语与英语之间存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 的总体多模态能力掩盖了不同认知层级上的深层缺陷，BloomBench 为构建更符合人类认知结构且更具跨语言包容性的 VLM 提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), the field lacks benchmarks that rigorously diagnose their true reasoning abilities and chart meaningful progress toward human-like multimodal intelligence. Most existing evaluations focus on piecemeal or disconnected tasks, obscuring critical cognitive weaknesses and providing little insight for targeted improvement. To address this gap, we introduce BloomBench, part of the Almieyar benchmarking series, the first cognitively human-grounded, bilingual (English-Arabic) multimodal benchmark for VLMs. Grounded in Bloom's Taxonomy, BloomBench systematically evaluates six levels of cognition (Remember, Understand, Apply, Analyze, Evaluate, Create) through carefully designed image-question-answer tasks. Built with a semi-automated pipeline and validated through a stratified hybrid quality assurance protocol, it ensures scalability, cultural inclusivity, and linguistic fidelity. Leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive study of state-of-the-art VLMs to diagnose their cognitive profiles. Our analysis reveals a sharp cognitive asymmetry: while state-of-the-art models achieve strong performance ceilings in semantic understanding, they struggle substantially with factual recall and creative synthesis. This demonstrates that current general multimodal proficiency masks deeper limitations in specific cognitive layers. Furthermore, our study highlights a critical performance gap between Arabic and English, exposing limitations in current cross-lingual multimodal reasoning. These findings establish a foundation for developing more cognitively aligned and inclusive VLMs. The benchmark framework and dataset is available at: https://github.com/qcri/Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench.

</details>


### [23] [Noise-Aware Visual Representation Learning for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05535)
*I Putu Adi Pratama,Bahadorreza Ofoghi,Atul Sajjanhar,Shang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向医学视觉问答的噪声感知框架，在视觉嵌入映射到大语言模型之前加入去噪自编码器，以提升对噪声输入的鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据上的竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Med-VQA 方法通常将现成视觉编码器通过轻量映射网络连接到 LLM，以降低计算成本，但往往忽视视觉表示中的噪声和微小无关扰动，导致模型鲁棒性不足。

Method: 先使用去噪自编码器从被扰动的视觉嵌入中重建干净嵌入，学习稳健视觉表示；随后通过多层感知机将嵌入投影到 LLM 的词嵌入空间，形成视觉前缀 token；模型适配阶段采用 LoRA 进行参数高效微调。

Result: 在 SLAKE 和 PathVQA 基准上评估，实验显示该方法在噪声视觉嵌入条件下具有更好的鲁棒性，同时在干净输入上的性能保持竞争力，并在多种评价指标上表现稳定。

Conclusion: 通过显式学习抗噪视觉表示，可有效增强 Med-VQA 系统在噪声扰动下的稳定性与可靠性，为临床决策支持场景中的鲁棒多模态问答提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) has strong potential for clinical decision support by enabling AI models to interpret medical images and answer clinically relevant queries. Recent approaches typically connect off-the-shelf vision encoders with large language models (LLMs) through lightweight mapping networks to reduce computational cost. However, these methods often overlook the importance of handling noise and small irrelevant changes in visual representations. To address these challenges, we propose a noise-aware Med-VQA framework that incorporates a denoising autoencoder before visual embeddings are mapped into the input space of an LLM. The denoising autoencoder is pretrained to reconstruct clean visual embeddings from corrupted inputs, encouraging the model to learn robust visual representations that are less sensitive to noise. The resulting embeddings are then projected into the language model embedding space using a multi-layer perceptron (MLP), forming visual prefix tokens that provide image information to the LLM. To enable efficient adaptation without full retraining, we employ parameter-efficient fine-tuning using low-rank adaptation (LoRA). The proposed method is evaluated on the SLAKE and PathVQA benchmarks. Experimental results show improved robustness to noisy input embeddings while maintaining competitive clean performance across multiple evaluation criteria. These findings suggest that learning more robust visual representations can enhance Med-VQA performance and robustness.

</details>


### [24] [Dual Feature Decoupling for Fine-Grained OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05536)
*Xiaokun Li,Yaping Huang,Qingji Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DFDNet，通过双重特征解耦提升细粒度任务中的分布外检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OOD 检测方法多假设类别间分布差异较大，难以应对细粒度场景中类别视觉差异微小、背景干扰强的问题，因此需要更适合细粒度识别的 OOD 检测方法。

Method: 提出 Dual Feature Decoupling Network（DFDNet），包含空间-频率解耦模块与重构引导解耦模块。前者保留分类判别性的内容特征并抑制任务无关风格信息；后者通过像素级对抗重构任务去除低层非判别信息，增强类别相关的高层语义表示。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法取得了具有竞争力的性能提升，尤其适用于细粒度 OOD 检测场景。

Conclusion: 通过从特征解耦角度建模细粒度 OOD 检测，DFDNet 能有效缓解细粒度类别相似性与背景干扰带来的挑战，提升 OOD 检测效果。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution detection (OOD) is an indispensable technique when applying machine learning models to real-world scenarios. Most existing OOD detection methods have been developed under the idealized assumption of large inter-class distributional differences, while largely overlooking fine-grained tasks characterized by subtle variations, such as medical image classification and vehicle recognition. The high visual similarity among fine-grained subcategories, together with the interference of background factors, makes OOD detection extremely challenging. To tackle this problem, we propose a novel Dual Feature Decoupling Network (DFDNet), which addresses fine-grained OOD detection from the perspective of feature disentanglement. The proposed DFDNet comprises two key components: a spatial-frequency decoupling module and a reconstruction-guided decoupling module. The spatial-frequency decoupling module is designed to preserve content features that are discriminative for classification while suppressing task-irrelevant style information. On the other hand, the reconstruction-guided decoupling module introduces a novel pixel-level adversarial reconstruction task to further remove low-level, non-discriminative information and enhance category-specific high-level semantic representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance improvements on multiple datasets.

</details>


### [25] [UltraVR: A Diagnostic Ultra-Resolution Image-VQA Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05576)
*Gexin Huang,Yanting Yang,Myeongkyun Kang,Beidi Zhao,Jun Zhou,Chen Zhou,Gang Wang,Zu-hua Gao,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UltraVR，一个面向超高分辨率图像的证据驱动视觉推理诊断基准，用于评估 VLM 是否真正定位、感知并整合关键视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在常规视觉问答和多模态推理任务上表现较好，但其在超高分辨率图像中处理微小、细微、远距离或分散证据的能力尚不清楚；现有评测多只关注最终答案准确率，难以揭示模型推理流程中具体失败环节。

Method: 作者构建 UltraVR 基准，覆盖 CCTV、遥感、全切片病理图像和工业异常检测四类场景。每个样本除标准问答三元组外，还提供结构化真实推理链，包括分步问题、中间答案和推理标签；标签将推理过程分解为证据定位、局部感知、量化、证据整合和决策推断，以支持过程级诊断。

Result: 对前沿 VLM 的评测显示，当前模型在超高分辨率视觉推理上仍不可靠。错误主要集中在证据定位和局部感知阶段；当提供中间视觉事实后，下游推断环节往往能够恢复较好表现。

Conclusion: UltraVR 不仅能衡量 VLM 最终是否答对，还能定位其超高分辨率推理流程中的失败位置，是一个用于诊断视觉到决策链路瓶颈的测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) excel on visual question answering and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Yet their capability on ultra-resolution images - where critical evidence is tiny, subtle, spatially distant, or distributed - remains unclear. Existing evaluations largely report final-answer accuracy, offering limited insight into whether models acquire and integrate the necessary visual evidence. We introduce UltraVR, a diagnostic benchmark for evidence-grounded visual reasoning over ultra-resolution images. UltraVR spans four high-value scenarios: CCTV surveillance, remote sensing (RS), whole-slide image (WSI) pathology, and industrial anomaly detection (AD). These domains pose complementary challenges: fine-grained object grounding in crowded CCTV scenes, long-range spatial comparison in RS, multi-scale evidence navigation in WSI, and subtle irregularity detection in repetitive industrial layouts. Beyond standard QA triples, each instance includes a structured ground-truth chain of thought with step-level questions, intermediate answers, and reasoning labels. These labels decompose reasoning into evidence grounding, local perception, quantification, evidence integration, and decision inference, enabling process-level diagnosis over black-box scoring. Using UltraVR, we evaluate frontier VLMs and show that current models remain far from reliable on ultra-resolution reasoning. Importantly, the structured annotations allow us to localize failures across the visual-to-decision pipeline: errors concentrate in evidence grounding and local perception, while downstream inference often recovers when intermediate visual facts are supplied. These findings demonstrate UltraVR as a diagnostic testbed for measuring not only whether VLMs answer correctly, but where their ultra-resolution reasoning process breaks.

</details>


### [26] [BMCR: Adaptive Backbone Module Composition via Reinforcement Learning for Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05586)
*Wenlin Liu,Xikun Hu,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BMCR通过强化学习动态组合CNN与ViT的可复用骨干模块，为不同复杂度的遥感图像自适应生成推理路径，在多个遥感目标检测数据集上取得更高mAP并保持较好效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN擅长局部细节建模，ViT擅长全局上下文建模，但现有检测器多依赖固定单一骨干或人工设计混合结构，难以针对不同输入复杂度自适应利用两类架构的互补优势。

Method: 提出Backbone Module Composition via Reinforcement Learning（BMCR）：构建包含CNN和ViT功能模块的可扩展模块工具箱，并为模块加入结构、语义和计算元数据以支持兼容装配；设计基于最优传输的轻量转换接口，对齐CNN网格特征与ViT token表示；将骨干组合建模为序列决策问题，由策略网络根据中间多尺度观测逐步选择模块；提出AMCO训练策略以协调模块更新、路由探索与奖励分配。

Result: 在DOTA-v1.0、DOTA-v1.5和DIOR-R上分别达到79.31%、73.41%和71.86% mAP，相比强静态与动态基线最高提升约2.5个百分点，同时保持有竞争力的效率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过强化学习驱动的跨架构模块级动态组合，可以更充分地融合CNN局部建模与ViT全局建模能力，提升遥感目标检测性能与输入自适应性。

Abstract: In remote sensing object detection, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) excel at capturing local details while Vision Transformers (ViTs) are better at global context modeling. However, existing detectors typically rely on a single fixed backbone or a manually designed hybrid architecture, and thus fail to adaptively exploit these complementary strengths across inputs of diverse complexity. To address this limitation, we propose Backbone Module Composition via Reinforcement Learning (BMCR). BMCR dynamically assembles input-adaptive inference paths from reusable modules decomposed from off-the-shelf CNN and ViT backbones. To enable such cross-family composition, we first construct an extensible module toolbox. Specifically, we decompose representative CNN and ViT backbones into reusable functional modules and encapsulate each module with explicit structural, semantic, and computational metadata for compatibility-aware assembly. To bridge the gap between grid-based CNN features and token-based ViT representations, we design a lightweight Optimal Transport (OT) based transition interface that ensures distribution-aware alignment while respecting spatial consistency. The backbone composition process is then formulated as a sequential decision problem, in which a policy network progressively selects task-relevant modules according to intermediate multi-scale observations. To stabilize the joint optimization of reusable modules and the routing policy, we further develop an Adaptive Module Cooperative Optimization (AMCO) strategy that coordinates module updating, routing exploration, and reward assignment during training. On DOTA-v1.0, DOTA-v1.5 and DIOR-R, BMCR achieves 79.31\%, 73.41\% and 71.86\% mAP, respectively, surpassing strong static and dynamic baselines by up to 2.5 points while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [27] [HDST-GNN: Heterogeneous Dynamic Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Networks for Multi-Object Tracking in UAV Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05587)
*Phillip Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HDST-GNN，用于无人机多目标跟踪，通过高度自适应建图、异构节点建模和遮挡门控时序聚合提升密集小目标与遮挡场景下的关联鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机多目标跟踪中存在高度变化、目标小且密集、遮挡频繁导致身份切换等问题；现有图跟踪方法通常假设固定空间上下文，并统一处理不同生命周期状态的目标，难以适应复杂动态场景。

Method: 提出异构动态时空图神经网络 HDST-GNN：用平均目标面积估计高度代理并自适应调整边连接半径；将检测框、已确认轨迹和丢失轨迹建模为不同类型节点并使用类型化边关系；根据遮挡置信度门控注意力贡献；结合可微 Sinkhorn 匹配头，以交叉熵和三元组损失端到端训练。

Result: 在 VisDrone2019-MOT oracle detections 设置下达到 94.51% MOTA 和 97.24% IDF1，相比 SORT 提升 5.0 个 MOTA 点，身份切换减少 81%；使用 YOLOv8n 实际检测时，相比 SORT 身份切换减少 49%；消融实验验证各模块均有独立贡献。

Conclusion: HDST-GNN 通过显式建模高度变化、目标生命周期异构性和遮挡影响，有效提升无人机多目标跟踪的身份一致性和整体性能，尤其适用于小目标密集和遮挡频繁场景。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) from UAV imagery presents unique challenges: altitude varies across sequences, objects are small and densely packed, and frequent occlusion causes identity switches. Existing graph-based trackers assume fixed spatial context and treat all objects uniformly, ignoring the heterogeneous lifecycle states of detections, active tracklets, and lost targets. We propose HDST-GNN, a Heterogeneous Dynamic Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Network with three novel contributions. First, Altitude-Adaptive Edge Construction estimates a camera-altitude proxy from mean object area and adjusts the graph connectivity radius accordingly. Second, Heterogeneous Node Representation models detections (Type-D), confirmed tracklets (Type-T), and lost tracklets (Type-L) as distinct node types with dedicated projections and typed edge relations. Third, Occlusion-Gated Temporal Aggregation gates each node's attention contribution by its occlusion confidence, preventing occluded nodes from corrupting neighbour embeddings. HDST-GNN is trained end-to-end with a differentiable Sinkhorn head using joint cross-entropy and triplet loss. On VisDrone2019-MOT with oracle detections, HDST-GNN achieves 94.51% MOTA and 97.24% IDF1, outperforming SORT by +5.0 MOTA points and reducing identity switches by 81%. With real YOLOv8n detections, HDST-GNN reduces identity switches by 49% vs. SORT. Ablation studies confirm the independent contribution of each component.

</details>


### [28] [What's Under the Skin? Estimating Swine Body Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05611)
*Mk Bashar,Kuljit Bhatti,Gary Rohrer,Madonna Benjamin,Tami Brown-Brandl,Daniel Morris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PigFormer利用顶置RGB-D相机的原始深度帧，经过几何标准化与切片注意力编码，实现母猪背膘、腰肌深度和总组织厚度的非接触自动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产中常用的目测评分和卡尺评估母猪体况与真实组织组成相关性较弱；超声虽能直接测量背膘和腰肌深度，但操作耗时、依赖人工，难以规模化部署。因此需要一种可连续、自动、非接触且适用于商业养殖环境的体况监测方法。

Method: 论文提出两阶段系统PigFormer。第一阶段为几何前端：将顶置RGB-D相机采集的原始深度帧转换为标准化高度图，包括SAM3到MaskDINO的分割蒸馏、地平面移除和姿态方向归一化。第二阶段为Slice Attention Encoder：将高度图表示为一系列横截面切片，并建模沿背部表面的空间关系，以预测最后肋骨处皮下背膘厚度、腰肌深度和总组织厚度。

Result: 在来自两个养殖设施、包含319个母猪和后备母猪实例的多站点数据集上，PigFormer取得2.43 mm背膘MAE和3.87 mm总体MAE，优于单阶段ResNet-18和ViT-small等强基线模型。

Conclusion: PigFormer证明了基于顶置RGB-D深度感知和结构化注意力建模的方案可有效替代高人工成本超声流程，为商业化猪场中的连续、自动、非接触母猪体况监测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Sow body condition is an important indicator for growers as it has a large impact on lactation performance and piglet survival. However, body condition measures used during production, such as visual scoring and calipers, correlate poorly with underlying tissue composition. Ultrasound scans can provide direct measurements of subcutaneous backfat thickness and loin muscle depth, but their operation is labor intensive and not scalable for production. We present PigFormer, an end-to-end two-stage system that takes raw depth frames from a ceiling-mounted RGB-D camera and predicts subcutaneous backfat thickness, loin muscle depth, and total tissue thickness at the last rib. Stage 1 is a geometric front-end that converts raw depth into a standardized height map via SAM3-to-MaskDINO segmentation distillation, ground-plane removal, and orientation normalization. Stage 2 is a Slice Attention Encoder that treats each height map as a sequence of cross-sectional slices and captures spatial relationships along the full dorsal surface. On a multi-site dataset of 319 sow and gilt instances from two facilities, PigFormer achieves 2.43 mm backfat MAE and 3.87 mm overall MAE. It outperforms strong single-stage ResNet-18 and ViT-small baselines. PigFormer offers a practical path toward continuous, automated, non-contact body condition monitoring in commercial swine production. Code is available at https://github.com/iambashar/Pigformer.

</details>


### [29] [KV-Control: Parameter-Efficient K/V Injection for Trajectory-Controlled Text-to-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05624)
*Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Xiaoyu Zhan,Yanwen Guo,Dongjie Fu,Xiaohao Cai,Hansung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 KV-Control，一种用于冻结的文本到3D人体运动Transformer的轻量级轨迹控制接口，通过在自注意力中注入控制条件的Key/Value记忆，实现高精度轨迹约束同时保持文本生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件3D人体运动模型能根据语言生成合理动作，但实际动画和具身智能场景还需要满足根节点路径、末端执行器目标或多关节轨迹等几何约束。现有方法要么复制大量生成器结构以获得逐层控制能力，要么依赖昂贵的测试时优化，难以兼顾精确控制、效率与预训练运动先验保持。

Method: 作者提出KV-Control，将几何约束作为自注意力中的记忆进行注入，而不是通过全局姿态token或仅在输出端施加约束。方法包括：PartVQ学习解剖结构对齐的身体部位代码本；T-Concat将每个帧-部位token暴露为可被注意力寻址的位置；KV-Control在每个自注意力层注入由控制条件生成的Key/Value记忆，同时冻结预训练查询流、文本交叉注意力、FFN和主干参数，仅训练少量注入参数及共享轨迹编码器。

Result: 该方法在继承的迭代细化协议下，对根节点和多关节轨迹约束实现亚厘米级跟踪精度，同时保持较好的文本条件运动质量。其适配器规模小，训练参数少，控制过程透明。

Conclusion: KV-Control将轨迹条件建模为轻量级记忆检索问题，为文本到运动生成提供了一种小型、精确且可解释的控制接口，适用于需要同时满足语言意图和几何约束的动画与具身智能工作流。

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D human motion models now synthesize plausible motions from prompts, but practical animation and embodied-agent workflows rarely stop at text: a character may need to follow a sketched root path, hit an end-effector target, or satisfy a multi-joint trajectory while still preserving the gait, style, and intent described by language. This exposes a control trade-off. A trajectory controller should be precise without overwriting the pretrained text-conditioned motion prior, yet existing solutions either duplicate large portions of the generator to regain per-layer control access or move much of the cost to test-time optimization. We introduce KV-Control, a compact attention-side control interface for frozen masked text-to-motion transformers. The key idea is to make geometric constraints available as memory inside self-attention rather than injecting them through a global pose token or enforcing them only at the output side. To support this interface, we co-design a part-tokenized motion substrate and controller: \textbf{PartVQ} learns anatomy-aligned part codebooks, T-Concat exposes each frame--part token as an attention-addressable site, and KV-Control injects control-conditioned key/value memories at every self-attention layer while preserving the pretrained query stream, text cross-attention, FFN, and all backbone weights. The resulting adapter adds only trainable injection parameters atop a shared trajectory encoder, yet tracks root and multi-joint constraints with sub-centimeter accuracy under the inherited refinement protocol while retaining text-conditioned motion quality. KV-Control reframes trajectory conditioning as lightweight memory retrieval, providing a small, precise, and transparent control interface for text-to-motion generation.

</details>


### [30] [ShotCrop$^3$: Cropping Human-Centric Images into Cinematic Triple-Shot Compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05635)
*Dehong Kong,Lina Lei,Lingtao Zheng,Chenyang Wu,Ailing Zhang,Xinran Qin,Teng Ma,Jiaqi Xu,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Xuecheng Qi,Renjing Pei,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三镜头构图任务 TSC，并设计 ShotCrop 模型，从单张以人为中心的图像生成建立镜头、中景、特写三类裁剪及简短描述，以支持视觉叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有审美构图研究通常只生成单个美观裁剪，忽视了同一场景中多镜头组合对叙事和商业创意工作流的价值；海报等应用往往需要不同侧重点的多张裁剪来呈现故事节奏。

Method: 作者提出 Triple-Shot Compositions 任务，并训练 ShotCrop。训练分三阶段：首先使用链式思维监督微调学习推理与审美裁剪能力；其次用结合多模态大模型评分、审美评估和 CLIP 相似度筛选出的高置信伪标签进行半监督微调；最后采用面向 ShotCrop 的 Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO-S），通过复合奖励进一步优化。

Result: 作者构建了包含 1.2k 专家标注测试样例的 TSC-Bench。实验显示，ShotCrop 在镜头定位准确性上相较 GPT-5 平均提升 2.82 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作将单图审美裁剪扩展为面向叙事的三镜头组合生成，并通过多阶段训练和高置信伪标签机制提升了多镜头裁剪质量，展示了在创意视觉工作流中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Prior work on aesthetic composition typically produces a single aesthetically pleasing crop, overlooking the narrative value of composing multiple shots from one scene. In practice, multi-shot composition is critical for downstream creative workflows: commercial posters often require multiple crops with different emphases (e.g., context, subject, and emotion/product details) to present key story beats. Therefore, we propose \textbf{Triple-Shot Compositions (TSC)}, a composition task that generates a three-shot set -- establishing, medium, and close-up -- from a single human-centric image, each paired with a brief shot description to support visual narration. To learn TSC with limited expert annotations, we introduce \textbf{ShotCrop} which undergoes a three-stage training process: it first applies Chain-of-Thought supervised fine-tuning to establish basic reasoning and aesthetic shot-cropping skills, then performs semi-supervised fine-tuning with high-confidence pseudo labels to further enhance aesthetic capability, and is finally optimized with Group Relative Policy Optimization for \textbf{ShotCrop} (GRPO-S) using a composite reward tailored for it. Specifically, our pseudo-labeling strategy combines MLLM-based scoring, aesthetic assessment, and CLIP similarity to retain high-confidence training signals. In addition, we present TSC-Bench, a benchmark of 1.2k expert-annotated test cases. Notably, ShotCrop achieves an average improvement of \textbf{2.82} times over GPT-5 in shot localization accuracy.

</details>


### [31] [Multi-Task Crack Foundation Model for Engineering-Reliable Crack Representation and Topology Preservation in Civil Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05641)
*Blessing Agyei Kyem,Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Eugene Denteh,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrackGeoFM 利用冻结视觉基础模型并加入裂缝领域适配，实现裂缝掩码、骨架与不确定性联合预测，在多数据集上提升分割精度、拓扑完整性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有裂缝分割模型虽然可能取得较高重叠指标，但常出现裂缝断裂、细小分支漏检，并且缺乏可靠的不确定性估计；在跨域场景下，预测稳定性和工程可用性不足。

Method: 提出多任务框架 CrackGeoFM：以冻结视觉基础模型作为骨干网络，结合裂缝特定适配模块。核心组件包括 FCEM 用于增强高频裂缝线索，CFAM 用于将冻结特征适配到裂缝领域模式，SMTD 用于联合解码裂缝掩码、骨架结构和不确定性。

Result: 在 20 个裂缝数据集上取得当前最优或领先的分割性能，同时改善拓扑保持能力，提供校准后的不确定性估计，并在仅 5 张标注图像的少样本设置下表现出有效适应能力。

Conclusion: 该方法提升了裂缝检测从像素分割到结构理解与可靠性评估的完整性，适合面向基础设施检测的可泛化、可信和工程化裂缝分析。

Abstract: Reliable crack assessment requires not only accurate pixel-level masks but also connected crack geometry and confidence estimates that remain stable under domain shift. However, existing segmentation models can achieve high overlap scores while fragmenting cracks, missing fine branches, and providing no calibrated uncertainty. To address this gap, this paper proposes CrackGeoFM, a multi-task framework that combines a frozen visual foundation backbone with crack-specific adaptation for mask prediction, skeleton reconstruction, and uncertainty estimation. The framework integrates a Frequency-Guided Crack Enhancement Module (FCEM) to enhance high-frequency crack cues, a Crack-Domain Feature Adaptation Module (CFAM) to adapt frozen backbone features to crack-domain patterns, and a Structure-Aware Multi-Task Decoder (SMTD) to jointly decode masks, skeletons, and uncertainty. Across 20 crack datasets, CrackGeoFM achieves state-of-the-art segmentation, improved topology preservation, calibrated uncertainty, and effective few-shot adaptation with only five labeled images. These results support reliable, generalizable, and engineering-oriented crack analysis for infrastructure assessment.

</details>


### [32] [CoFi-UCGen: Coarse-to-Fine Unsupervised Conditional Generation without Label Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05652)
*Shengxi Li,Zhaokun Hu,Ce Zheng,Mai Xu,Jingyuan Xia,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CoFi-UCGen：一种无标签的由粗到细条件图像生成框架，通过显式解耦全局语义与细粒度变化，提升无监督条件生成的质量、一致性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督条件图像生成难以在没有人工标签的情况下获得结构化、跨粒度一致的语义表示，导致条件控制不稳定、语义粒度混杂、细粒度属性难以准确操控。

Method: 论文提出对抗语义互易学习以约束图像空间与潜空间之间的语义一致性和完整性；设计 bit-codes 构建结构化粗粒度潜空间，并证明其可表达不同全局语义且保持独立噪声采样；在此基础上构建细粒度语义基，并在扩散模型中引入层级调制机制，实现由粗条件到细属性的逐层控制。

Result: 实验表明，在不使用标签先验或预训练特征提取器的条件下，CoFi-UCGen 在图像质量、语义一致性和控制准确性方面持续优于已有 UCGen 方法。

Conclusion: 显式的粗到细语义分解能够有效解决无监督条件图像生成中的语义结构化与可控性问题，为无标签条件生成提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Unsupervised conditional image generation (UCGen) aims to control generation without relying on manually annotated labels, yet remains challenging due to unstructured semantic representations across granularities. To address this, we propose a novel coarse-to-fine UCGen framework (CoFi-UCGen) that explicitly disentangles global semantics from fine-grained variations, which to the best of our knowledge, sets out the first successful attempt for both coarse- and fine-grained conditional generation without any labels. More specifically, we first propose the adversarial semantic reciprocal learning theory to ensure the semantic consistency and completeness between images and latent spaces. Based on the consistency, we propose the bit-codes to learn a structured coarse-grained latent space, and further prove distinct global semantics inherent from our bit-codes while preserving independent noise sampling for generation. Building upon these bit-codes, we establish a fine-grained semantic basis and introduce a hierarchical modulation mechanism in diffusion models, by enabling layer-wise injection from coarse conditions to progressively control fine-grained attributes during generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that without any label priors or pre-trained feature extractors, our CoFi-UCGen consistently outperforms existing UCGen methods in terms of image quality, semantic consistency, and control accuracy, verifying the effectiveness of explicit coarse-to-fine semantic decomposition for the challenging UCGen task.

</details>


### [33] [V2V-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Video-to-Video Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05665)
*Tao Liu,Leela Krishna,Gouti Pavan Kumar,Sreeja K,Vishav Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 V2V-Bench，用于评估视频到视频生成在指令遵循与源视频帧级对应保持方面的能力，并验证其与人工评价高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频和图像到视频评测指标难以衡量视频到视频生成的核心要求：既要按照编辑指令完成变换，又要保留与源视频的时间、结构和语义对应关系。

Method: 构建包含 5 大类、11 个维度的 V2V-Bench，覆盖时间对齐、结构保真、变换质量、视频质量和语义对齐；将多样化源视频与具有挑战性的编辑任务配对，并评估 Grok Imagine、Gemini Veo3 和 Open Sora 2。

Result: 实验显示不同模型具有互补优势：Grok 在编辑保真度方面表现更好，Veo3 在视觉质量方面更强；在 6 个 V2V 特定维度上，V2V-Bench 与人工判断的 Spearman 相关系数达到 0.905。

Conclusion: V2V-Bench 能较好捕捉视频到视频生成的关键评价需求，并可作为衡量模型编辑能力、视觉质量及与人工偏好一致性的有效基准。

Abstract: Video-to-video (V2V) generation is difficult to evaluate because outputs must both follow editing instructions and preserve frame-level correspondence with the source video, which existing T2V and I2V metrics do not capture. We introduce V2V-Bench, a 11-dimension benchmark organized into five categories: temporal alignment, structural fidelity, transformation quality, video quality, and semantic alignment. V2V-Bench pairs diverse source videos with challenging editing tasks and evaluates two commercial models, Grok Imagine and Gemini Veo3, and one open-source model, Open Sora 2. Results show complementary model strengths: Grok performs better on editing fidelity, while Veo3 achieves stronger visual quality. On six V2V-specific dimensions, V2V-Bench reaches a Spearman correlation of 0.905 with human judgments.

</details>


### [34] [LongSpace: Exploring Long-Horizon Spatial Memory from Perception to Recall in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05677)
*Shiqiang Lang,Jing Liu,Haoyang He,Peiwen Sun,Yuanteng Chen,Tao Liu,Lan Yang,Longteng Guo,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LongSpace-Bench 用于评估长视频中的空间记忆能力，并提出 LongSpace 记忆框架，通过分块建模、3D结构提示和层感知记忆检索提升多模态大语言模型的长时程空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在图像和视频理解上进展明显，但长时程任务不仅需要理解当前画面，还需要记住并检索过去观察到的空间布局、路径、视角变化和物体状态。因此，需要专门评估和增强模型的长视频空间记忆能力。

Method: 作者构建 LongSpace-Bench，一个基于房间游览视频的长时程空间记忆基准，覆盖场景感知、空间关系和空间记忆。同时提出 LongSpace 框架：将长视频建模为连续片段，在解码器早期层融合3D结构线索，并构建层感知记忆机制，以支持面向问题的相关记忆检索。

Result: 在多个空间推理基准上的实验表明，LongSpace 能提升长视频空间理解与推理表现，尤其增强了模型对历史空间信息的记忆和调用能力。

Conclusion: 显式空间记忆是长时程视频多模态大语言模型的关键能力；通过结构化视频分块、3D空间线索注入和问题引导的层级记忆检索，可以有效提升模型在复杂长视频空间推理任务中的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced image and video understanding and can increasingly handle longer visual inputs. Long-horizon tasks such as autonomous driving and robotic navigation require more than recognizing the current view, as models must remember and retrieve previously observed spatial layouts, routes, viewpoint changes, and object states. To evaluate this capability, we introduce LongSpace-Bench, a room-tour video benchmark for long-horizon spatial memory, covering scene perception, spatial relations, and spatial memory. In this work, we further propose LongSpace, a memory framework for long-video spatial reasoning. LongSpace models long videos as sequential chunks, incorporates 3D structural cues into early decoder layers, and constructs layer-aware memory for question-guided retrieval. Experiments on multiple spatial reasoning benchmarks show that LongSpace improves long-video spatial understanding, further demonstrating explicit spatial memory as a key capability for long-horizon video MLLMs.

</details>


### [35] [T-SAR-JEPA: Self-Supervised Temporal Anomaly Detection in SAR Amplitude Stacks via Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05700)
*Kerod Woldesenbet,Abem Woldesenbet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-SAR-JEPA 是一个用于 SAR 振幅时间序列异常检测的自监督潜在预测框架，在 DFC 2026 数据集的火山喷发时间窗上显著优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR 时间序列异常检测需要在缺乏人工标注的情况下识别时序变化；作者希望仅利用 SAR 振幅数据进行建模，并用独立的 InSAR coherence 作为伪真值评估检测质量。

Method: 先将 SAR-JEPA 的 ViT-Base/16 编码器通过局部掩码重建和梯度特征预测在 39,300 个 Capella patch 上进行领域自适应；随后使用带正弦时间编码的 temporal transformer，从 K=7 次观测预测未来潜在状态，并采用 progressive unfreezing 降低验证损失。

Result: 在 DFC 2026 数据集（300 条时间序列、3 个 AOI）上，T-SAR-JEPA 在 Hawaii eruption window 达到 77.0% ROC-AUC，优于 RX、PaDiM、Linear AR 和 LSTM 等约 50% 的基线；检测结果具有 99.9% 空间一致性，置换检验 p < 0.001。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于潜在状态预测的自监督时序建模可在仅使用 SAR 振幅的条件下有效检测结构化时序异常，并在独立伪真值评估中表现出较强可靠性。

Abstract: We present T-SAR-JEPA, a self-supervised framework for temporal anomaly detection in SAR amplitude stacks via latent prediction. A ViT-Base/16 encoder from SAR-JEPA is domain-adapted on 39,300 Capella patches using local masked reconstruction with gradient feature prediction. A temporal transformer with sinusoidal time encoding forecasts future latent states from K=7 acquisitions, with progressive unfreezing substantially reducing validation loss. The model operates on amplitude alone; InSAR coherence serves exclusively as independent pseudo-ground-truth. On the DFC 2026 dataset (300 time-series, three AOIs), T-SAR-JEPA achieves ROC-AUC of 77.0% on the Hawaii eruption window, outperforming RX, PaDiM, Linear AR, and LSTM baselines (~50%). Spatial coherence of 99.9% (p < 0.001, permutation test) confirms structured detections. Code: https://github.com/TerraLatent/t-sar-jepa

</details>


### [36] [Parallel Jacobi Decoding for Fast Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05703)
*Boya Liao,Ying Li,Siyong Jian,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的并行 Jacobi 解码方法，用二维空间域扩展草稿 token，加速自回归图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像生成质量高，但逐 token 顺序预测导致推理速度慢；已有 Jacobi 式解码在延长一维草稿序列后容易因误差传播导致加速收益饱和。

Method: 提出 Parallel Jacobi Decoding（PJD），利用图像局部空间相关性，在二维空间域并行扩展与细化草稿 token，并通过调整 attention mask 缓解误差累积、提升收敛稳定性。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种自回归图像生成模型上，PJD 实现了 4.8×–6.4× 的推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: PJD 通过空间并行化改进 Jacobi 解码，能够在无需额外训练的情况下显著提升自回归图像生成推理效率，并较好维持图像质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models have demonstrated remarkable performance in generating high-fidelity images. However, their inherently sequential next-token prediction leads to significantly slower inference. Recent studies have introduced Jacobi-style decoding to accelerate autoregressive image generation.
