### [205] [A Comprehensive Survey on Semantic Communication in Non-Terrestrial Networks: Architectures, Methodologies, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05216)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Avi Deb Raha,Huy Q. Le,Zhu Han,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述语义通信在第六代无线非地面网络中的适配性与研究进展，强调其可缓解带宽、时延、多普勒、能耗与链路不稳定等关键限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非地面网络可扩展覆盖范围，但面临长距离路径损耗、传播时延、动态多普勒、可见窗口受限以及星载/机载资源紧张等问题；语义通信通过传输任务相关意义而非原始比特，有望针对性解决这些瓶颈。

Method: 论文采用综述方法：先逐项分析NTN限制并匹配相应SemCom设计特性，再按NTN平台、语义方法与支撑技术三条轴线梳理文献，并分别讨论卫星、UAV/HAPS以及集成SAGIN系统。

Result: 综述表明，语义通信的高压缩率、深度联合源信道编码与生成式AI重建能力，能够分别缓解NTN中的带宽稀缺、低信噪比链路退化和遮挡/衰落下的信息恢复问题。

Conclusion: 文章认为SemCom与NTN具有高度互补性，并指出未来研究方向包括基础模型应用、能量感知调度、标准化缺口弥补以及面向深空通信的量子辅助语义通信。

Abstract: The sixth-generation wireless networks are envisioned to deliver ubiquitous, seamless, and intelligent connectivity that reaches far beyond the limits of terrestrial infrastructure. Non-terrestrial networks (NTNs) are central to this vision, extending coverage to underserved regions, remote terrain, and disaster zones that terrestrial deployment cannot economically reach. However, NTN architecture faces numerous limitations: severe path loss over long distances, long propagation delays, large and time-varying Doppler shifts, limited visibility windows, and tight on-board energy and computing budgets. Semantic communication (SemCom), which conveys the meaning of data rather than its raw bit-level representation, is unusually well matched to these conditions: extreme compression rate for task-oriented eases bandwidth scarcity, deep joint source-channel coding prevents the cliff effect due to low signal-to-noise ratio, and generative-AI reconstructs content from sparse cues that survive rain-faded or blocked links. This observation, that each NTN limitation maps onto a SemCom property that addresses it, motivates our survey. We first walk through the NTN limitations one by one, pairing each with the SemCom design choices that complement it, then we organize the literature along three axes: the NTN platform, the semantic methodology, and the supporting techniques, and follow this with platform-by-platform deep dives on satellite-centric, UAV/HAPS-centric, and integrated SAGIN systems. The survey concludes by identifying open research problems, gaps in existing standards, and future directions, including the application of foundation models, energy-aware scheduling, and quantum-assisted SemCom for deep space communication.

</details>


### [206] [Bounded Deep Unfolding for Joint Beamforming and Scheduling in Multi-Cell MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05246)
*Jiansheng Li,Shuqi Chai,Fan Xu,Kaiming Shen,Guangxu Zhu,Junting Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种联合深度展开框架，将模型驱动的 FastFP 优化与可学习模块结合，用于多小区 MIMO 下行网络中的 RBG 调度与波束成形联合优化，以提升加权和速率并降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FastFP 框架虽能可靠求解 WSR 最大化问题，但连续波束成形更新较保守，离散 RBG 匹配阶段计算开销高，尤其 Hungarian 匹配在大规模场景下复杂度较高。

Method: 提出包含 P-Net 与 K-Net 的联合深度展开框架。P-Net 在 FastFP 解析更新方向上学习自适应松弛因子，并将其限制在保证上升性的区间内；K-Net 学习长时域优先级策略，引导低复杂度贪婪 RBG 分配，替代高复杂度 Hungarian 匹配。两者利用算法先验和循环参数共享以支持超出训练长度的推理。

Result: 仿真显示，该框架相较传统模型驱动基线获得更高 WSR 和更快执行速度，并能在未见过的网络规模、天线配置和信道条件下保持较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合解析优化先验与深度展开学习可以在保证部分理论性质的同时显著提升无线资源调度与波束成形联合优化的效率和性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates the joint resource block group (RBG) scheduling and beamforming optimization problem for weighted sum-rate (WSR) maximization in multi-cell multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) downlink networks. While the Fast Fractional Programming (FastFP) framework provides a reliable model-driven solution, it suffers from conservative continuous beamforming updates and prohibitive computational overhead during the discrete RBG matching phase. To address these bottlenecks, we propose a joint deep unfolding framework comprising two core modules: P-Net and K-Net. For continuous beamforming, P-Net learns an adaptive relaxation factor along the analytical FastFP update direction. By strictly constraining this factor within an ascent-preserving interval, P-Net accelerates the optimization trajectory while rigorously retaining monotonic improvement and stationary-point convergence guarantees. For discrete RBG scheduling, K-Net learns a long-horizon priority policy that guides a low-complexity greedy assignment, effectively preserving the assignment quality while bypassing the high complexity of Hungarian matching. Both networks leverage analytical algorithmic priors and utilize recurrent parameter sharing, enabling flexible inference beyond the training horizon. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed joint framework achieves higher WSR and faster execution times than conventional model-driven baselines, while generalizing robustly across unseen network scales, antenna configurations, and channel conditions without retraining.

</details>


### [207] [X-Band UAV-enabled Integrated Sensing and Communications for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05262)
*Remon Polus,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究车联网中无人机赋能的集成感知与通信（ISaC）系统，在X波段条件下如何在感知与通信之间进行最优时间分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机可同时承担空中感知与通信平台角色，但在实际信道衰落、目标距离和无人机约束下，感知精度与通信性能之间存在资源分配冲突，需要建立可自适应权衡的优化机制。

Method: 论文考虑单阴影与双阴影信道模型，构建时间分配优化框架，在满足最低通信速率和感知可靠性约束的前提下，将系统时隙在感知任务与通信任务之间进行优化分配，并通过仿真分析不同信道条件和目标距离下的策略变化。

Result: 仿真表明，最优时间分配会随无人机到地面链路条件、信道阴影效应以及目标距离变化而自适应调整；当通信链路较差或目标更难感知时，系统需要相应增加通信或感知时间以满足约束。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了UAV辅助ISaC在智能交通场景中的可行性，并揭示了在实际空地信道条件下感知-通信资源权衡的重要性，为车联网中的无人机部署和资源调度提供了参考。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly considered as aerial platforms capable of providing both sensing and communication services, representing a promising paradigm for intelligent transportation systems. This paper investigates the optimal time allocation for a UAV-enabled integrated sensing and communication (ISaC) system operating in the X-band for vehicular networks. We analyze the trade-off between sensing accuracy and communication performance under practical UAV constraints and fading effects, considering both single-shadowing and double-shadowing channel models. An optimization framework is developed to allocate time between sensing and communication while guaranteeing minimum communication rates and sufficient sensing reliability. Simulation results demonstrate adaptive time allocation strategies, highlighting how UAV-to-ground channel conditions and target distances influence the balance between sensing and communication in smart mobility scenarios.

</details>


### [208] [Unlocking Exponential and Unbounded Robust Gains in Shannon Capacity of Classical Multiple Access Channels with Causal CSIT via Quantum Entanglement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05412)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究量子纠缠辅助在带因果信道状态信息的经典多用户接入信道中可带来的容量增益，发现其相对无纠缠情形可实现随用户数指数增长或随状态字母表规模无界增长的乘性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明量子纠缠辅助可提升经典通信网络的 Shannon 容量，但已知最大增益较小（低于 6%）。本文旨在回答：在合适的经典网络模型中，量子纠缠辅助是否可能带来显著甚至巨大的容量提升。

Method: 作者构造并分析若干具有因果信道状态信息的 K 用户经典多址接入信道，限定输入、输出和状态字母表可为固定二元规模；比较有无发送端量子纠缠辅助时的容量，并进一步考察用户数 K、状态字母表大小以及量子资源噪声对容量优势的影响。

Result: 对于某些固定二元输入、输出和状态字母表的 K 用户信道，纠缠辅助带来的乘性容量优势随 K 指数增长。对于 K=3 且输入输出仍为二元的情形，当状态字母表增大时，该优势可无界增长。具体例子中，K=5 和 K=7 时增益因子分别超过 21 和 88。即使每个纠缠量子比特以约 30% 概率完全退相干，指数级容量优势仍然存在。

Conclusion: 量子纠缠辅助不仅能小幅提升经典通信容量，在具有因果信道状态信息的多用户接入信道中还可产生巨大且鲁棒的乘性容量增益；并且这些增益仅需发送端之间共享量子纠缠即可实现。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement assistance is known to improve the Shannon capacity of classical communication networks but the largest gains noted thus far are rather modest (less than 6%), motivating the question: are large capacity gains ever possible? It is shown in this work that in the presence of causal channel state information at the transmitters, quantum entanglement assistance provides a multiplicative capacity advantage that grows exponentially with the number of users K for certain classical K-user multiple access channels with fixed size (binary) alphabet for inputs, outputs and states. Similarly, in the presence of causal channel state information at the transmitters, quantum entanglement assistance is shown to provide a multiplicative capacity advantage that is unbounded as the size of the state alphabet grows, while the number of users (K=3) and the input and output alphabet (binary) are held fixed. Even with only a few users and small alphabet sizes, substantial multiplicative gains in capacity are found, e.g., with binary inputs, outputs and states, multiplicative gains by factors exceeding 21 and 88 are noted with K=5 and K=7 users, respectively. The gains are robust in the sense that they persist even with noisy quantum resources, e.g., an exponential (in K) capacity advantage from quantum entanglement assistance remains available even if each entangled qubit independently depolarizes completely with probability $\approx$ 30%. The gains are based on quantum entanglement assistance provided only to the transmitters.

</details>


### [209] [Sequence Reconstruction for Substitution Channel: New Sufficient Conditions and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05454)
*Chen Wang,Eitan Yaakobi,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究序列重构问题，提出一种新的唯一重构充分条件：不仅考虑 noisy reads 的数量，还考虑 reads 之间的距离，并给出理论分析与高效重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Levenshtein 相关工作主要关注保证唯一重构所需的最小 reads 数量，这是一种较单一的充分条件。作者希望从更细粒度角度刻画唯一重构，将 reads 之间的相互距离信息纳入判断，以获得新的重构框架。

Method: 构建新的唯一重构框架，将 reads 数量与 reads 间距离共同作为判定条件；在该框架下进行理论推导，证明相应充分条件，并设计匹配的高效序列重构算法。

Result: 论文声称给出了新的唯一重构充分条件，并提供了相应的理论分析与可执行的高效重构算法。摘要未提供具体复杂度、界限改进幅度或实验结果。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了传统仅基于 reads 数量的唯一重构分析方式，表明 reads 间距离结构可用于提升或细化序列唯一重构的判定与算法设计。

Abstract: In the sequence reconstruction problem, a codeword $\x$ is transmitted through several identical channels where each channel produces a noisy read of $\x$, and the problem is to analyze how to uniquely reconstruct $\x$ based on these noisy reads. Levenshtein has studied the minimum number of reads which guarantees unique reconstruction of $\x$, which is one sufficient condition for unique reconstruction. In this paper, we move on to a different perspective and propose a new framework for unique reconstruction. Our new sufficient condition for unique reconstruction takes both the number of reads and the distances among the reads into consideration. We offer both theoretical analysis and corresponding efficient reconstruction algorithms for our reconstruction framework.

</details>


### [210] [Robust Repair of Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05573)
*Wilton Kim,Stanislav Kruglik,Gaojun Luo,Han Mao Kiah*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 Reed–Solomon 码在低通信带宽下单节点擦除修复的鲁棒性，将下载的 trace 集合视为 repair-trace code，并给出其纠错能力与维数/最小距离之间的理论界及高效修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guruswami–Wootters trace repair 框架可降低 RS 码单擦除修复的通信量，但实际分布式存储中 helper 节点可能返回错误信息，因此需要理解在低带宽修复过程中能否抵抗错误响应，以及可纠正错误数与码参数之间的关系。

Method: 作者把 helper 节点下载的 traces 组成一个新的码（repair-trace code），通过分析关联多项式的零系数结构，并用 cyclotomic cosets 对这些零系数进行刻画，推导允许纠正给定错误 traces 数 e 的维数 k 上界，以及关于 k 的最小距离下界；在 q=2 情形下利用 cyclotomic coset 代表元的显式公式得到单错误纠正的精确最优维数界；同时提出两种鲁棒修复算法，一种基于 BCH bound，另一种利用 character sums 的更强界。

Result: 论文给出了 repair-trace code 的参数界：包括支持 e 个错误 trace 纠正的 k 上界、最小距离随 k 变化的下界；对 q=2 的单错误纠正场景得到精确最优维数界。算法上，第一种方案达到 BCH bound 保证的纠错能力，第二种方案可接近基于 character sums 的更强纠错界，但计算复杂度增加一个 n 因子。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了低带宽 RS 码 trace repair 的鲁棒纠错理论，说明通过将修复下载信息编码化分析，可以在通信效率与错误容忍之间取得可证明权衡，并提供了可实现的高效鲁棒修复方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of robust repair of a single erasure in Reed--Solomon codes under low communication bandwidth. Focusing on the Guruswami--Wootters trace repair framework, we investigate whether a failed node can be correctly repaired in the presence of erroneous responses from helper nodes. Equivalently, we view the collection of downloaded traces as a code, which we call the repair-trace code. By characterizing the zero coefficients of the associated polynomial in terms of cyclotomic cosets, we derive upper bounds on the dimension $k$ that allow correction of a given number of erroneous traces $e$, as well as lower bounds on the minimum distance as a function of $k$. For the case $q=2$, we exploit explicit formulas for cyclotomic coset representatives to obtain the exact optimal dimension bound for single-error correction. We also propose two efficient robust repair schemes. Our first scheme achieves the error-correction capability guaranteed by the BCH bound. To approach a stronger bound based on character sums, we develop a second scheme that tolerates more errors at the cost of an additional factor $n$ in computational complexity.

</details>


### [211] [Energy Efficiency Optimization for Rotatable Antenna-Enabled Uplink NOMA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05600)
*Yixuan Li,Jun Wang,Hongbo Xu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究可旋转天线辅助的上行NOMA系统，通过联合优化接收波束成形、用户功率分配与天线旋转角度来最大化能效，并提出BCD框架下的迭代算法，仿真显示其能效优于基准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地面用户与空中用户共存的上行通信场景中，传统固定天线和常规NOMA方案在空间自由度与能效提升方面存在限制。可旋转天线可通过调整方向增强信道匹配和干扰管理，因此有必要研究其与NOMA结合后的能效优化潜力。

Method: 构建RA使能的多用户上行NOMA系统模型，基站配置多个可独立旋转的接收天线。论文将能效最大化表述为联合优化问题，变量包括接收波束成形、用户发射功率和RA旋转。为处理非凸性，提出基于块坐标下降的算法：接收波束成形采用最小均方误差准则更新；功率分配利用分式规划处理；RA旋转通过逐次凸近似求解。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提出的RA-NOMA方案在能效方面优于若干基准方案，说明可旋转天线与NOMA联合设计能够有效提升系统能量利用效率。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在上行NOMA系统中引入可旋转天线并进行联合资源优化的有效性。所提BCD迭代算法能够较好地处理复杂耦合优化问题，并在地空融合用户场景下实现更高能效。

Abstract: This paper investigates a rotatable antenna (RA)-enabled uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) system, where a base station equipped with multiple independently RAs serves both ground and aerial users. Specifically, we formulate an energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem by jointly optimizing receive beamforming, user power allocation, and RA rotation. To make the problem tractable, a new block coordinate descent-based algorithm is developed, in which the receive beamforming is updated via the minimum mean square error criterion, while the power allocation and RA rotation are handled by fractional programming and successive convex approximation. Numerical results demonstrate the EE superiority of the proposed RA-NOMA scheme over several benchmarks.

</details>


### [212] [Automated Proving of Shannon-Type Entropy Inequalities via Fine-Tuned Language Models and Guided Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05729)
*Shing Yin Wong,Shaocheng Liu,Linqi Song,Amin Gohari,Cheuk Ting Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究用小规模微调语言模型结合引导式束搜索，自动证明 Shannon 型熵不等式；0.6B 模型在测试集上达到较高成功率，并显著优于零样本大模型与传统工具基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon 型熵不等式证明需要将已知约束构造成非平凡线性组合，本质上是随随机变量数量增长而迅速恶化的组合搜索问题，因此需要更高效的自动化证明方法。

Method: 作者将 0.6B--1.7B 参数的小型语言模型在原子证明步骤上微调，并与 guided beam search 结合；在 n=10 到 15 变量的 60 个不等式测试集上评估，同时进行训练上下文长度、数据分布和束搜索评分策略的消融实验。

Result: 0.6B 微调模型配合树搜索达到 85% 证明成功率；GPT-5.5 零样本仅解决 1.7%，Psitip 解决 33.3%。消融显示 4096-token、非偏斜训练分布效果最佳；随机评分使成功率从 83% 降至 23%。

Conclusion: 小规模微调模型结合结构化搜索能够有效自动化 Shannon 型熵不等式证明；性能关键不在于更长上下文或偏斜数据，而在于合适的数据分布、可靠的步骤生成与有效的束搜索评分启发式。

Abstract: Proving Shannon-type entropy inequalities is a fundamental task in information theory that often requires constructing non-trivial linear combinations of known constraints, which is a combinatorial search problem that scales poorly with the number of random variables. We investigate whether small-scale large language models (0.6B--1.7B parameters), fine-tuned on atomic proof steps and combined with guided beam search, can automate this process. On a held-out test set of 60 inequalities spanning n=10 to 15 variables, our 0.6B fine-tuned model achieves an 85\% proof success rate with tree search. GPT-5.5 solves 1.7\% samples under zero-shot prompting while Psitip solves 33.3\% samples. A systematic ablation study across training context length (4096 vs.\ 8192 tokens) and data distribution (n=9-skewed vs not skewed) reveals that a 4096-token not skewed training distribution yields the best performance, with extended context and skewed data providing no marginal benefit. We further identify two dominant failure modes -- format failures and step quality degradation -- and verify that the beam-scoring heuristic is essential via a controlled ablation (random scoring reduces success from 83\% to 23\%).

</details>


### [213] [Compositional Boundaries for Density Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05871)
*Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Ali Darijani,Jürgen Beyerer,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究分布式不确定性管理中加权概率密度的二元融合何时可按层级树执行且不依赖合并顺序，核心结论是：在连续、加性权重、系数仅依赖权重的局部线段值融合规则中，只有归一化加权线性池化满足这种组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统常受通信、隐私或调度约束，需要沿聚合树逐步合并局部概率模型。作者关注最终融合密度是否只由源模型及其权重决定，而不受中间节点合并顺序影响，即层级执行的顺序不变性问题。

Method: 将二元加权概率密度融合形式化为代数组合性问题，分析局部线段值融合规则；在连续性、输出权重加性、融合系数仅依赖权重等假设下刻画可组合规则；进一步比较范数诱导的线段平衡、端点到候选的 f-散度平衡、全局散度重心以及高斯混合模型中的逐步压缩。

Result: 结果表明，满足层级顺序不变性的规则被刻画为归一化加权线性池化，范数诱导的线段平衡可实现相应系数。平滑端点到候选 f-散度平衡的二阶展开会导出平方根型有效权重，因此仅有局部两两可解并不足以保证调度无关。全局散度重心仍保持加性权重的局部极限。高斯混合模型中，精确融合具组合性，而逐步压缩只有在未归一化分量测度满足同余条件时才具组合性。

Conclusion: 论文区分了严格的、调度无关的精确融合与全局聚合目标、局部近似启发式之间的差异，给出了局部融合规则可层级组合的边界，并解释了若干常见平衡/压缩方法为何可能破坏顺序不变性。

Abstract: Distributed uncertainty-management systems often combine local probabilistic models along aggregation trees chosen by communication, privacy, or scheduling constraints. The final density should depend on the weighted sources, not on the particular order in which intermediate nodes combine them. We study this requirement as an algebraic compositionality problem for binary fusion of weighted probability densities. The central question is when a local fusion rule can be executed hierarchically while remaining order-invariant. We establish a compositional boundary for local segment-valued fusion rules. Within the class of continuous binary rules with additive output weights and weight-only coefficients, order-invariant hierarchical execution characterizes normalized weighted linear pooling; norm-induced segment balancing realizes the corresponding coefficient. Smooth endpoint-to-candidate $f$-divergence balancing has a different local geometry: its quadratic expansion induces square-root effective weights, showing why pairwise solvability alone is insufficient for schedule-independent fusion. We show that this obstruction is local to endpoint-to-candidate binary balancing, whereas global divergence barycenters retain additive-weight local limits. Finally, Gaussian mixtures show how the same issue appears in finite model classes: exact fusion is compositional, whereas stepwise compression is compositional only under a congruence condition on unnormalized component measures. These results distinguish exact schedule-independent fusion from global aggregation objectives and local approximation heuristics.

</details>


### [214] [Double-Directional Wireless Channel Modeling Using Statistics-Aided Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05993)
*Richmond Boamah,Ferdous Pervej*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统计辅助的机器学习方法，用于生成未来双向无线信道的Top-M多径分量，实现更长时间尺度且统计一致的信道建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向无线信道模型能提供完整传播信息，但现有随机/确定性模型与机器学习方法存在局限：多数ML方案关注短期信道 realization 对齐，时间跨度短、统计意义不足；同时多径分量数量会随接收端或交互物体的时空变化而变化，导致固定输入输出形状的典型ML模型难以适配。

Method: 作者提出统计辅助ML框架：首先从完整多径分量中选择功率等指标最重要的Top-M个MPC，其中M远小于总MPC数；然后基于这些MPC构建可学习图结构，并训练混合TimesNet-TimeFilter（TNTF）模型；最后通过信道统计辅助训练，使生成的未来Top-M双向信道 realization 的统计量尽可能接近完整时变双向信道的真实统计量。

Result: 在基于随机信道模型（SCM）的合成数据集和基于确定性射线追踪的数据集上进行了大量仿真验证。结果显示，该方法相较现有先进基线具有更好的有效性，尤其体现在未来信道生成及统计一致性方面。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将固定Top-M MPC选择、图学习结构和统计辅助训练结合，可以缓解MPC数量变化和短期预测局限，为真实系统设计中的双向无线信道建模提供更稳健的机器学习方案。

Abstract: The double-directional (DD) wireless channel model is important for realistic system design since it provides complete propagation information. While stochastic and deterministic channel models are widely adopted, and existing machine learning (ML) solutions mostly aim to align future channel realizations, these solutions are often limited to short time spans that may not be statistically significant. Moreover, because the number of multi-path components (MPCs) varies with spatial and temporal variation of the receiver (RX) and/or interacting objects (IOs), typical ML solutions that require fixed, predefined input and output shapes fall short. To curb these limitations, we propose a statistics-aided ML solution that relies on a fixed subset of MPCs selection. More specifically, we first select top-$M$ MPCs, where $M\in\mathbb{Z}^+$ is much smaller than the total number of MPCs, and construct learnable graphs to train our proposed hybrid TimesNet-TimeFilter (TNTF) model. We then use a channel statistics-aided training method to generate future top-M DD channel realizations such that the statistics calculated from these realizations matches closely with those of the actual statistics from the complete time-varying DD channel realizations. We validate the proposed solution using extensive simulations on both synthetic stochastic channel model (SCM)-based and deterministic ray-tracing-based datasets, and demonstrate its effectiveness relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [215] [Adapting Diffusion Language Models for Lossless Pixel-Level Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06273)
*Tianqi Ren,Rongpeng Li,Xianfu Chen,Yingyu Li,Zhifeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出 DDM-SSCC：一种基于离散扩散模型的分离式源信道编码框架，用于噪声信道下的无损像素级图像传输，并在多个数据集和信道条件下优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损像素级图像传输要求接收端精确恢复图像，既依赖高精度的符号概率建模，也依赖噪声信道中的可靠传输。传统光栅顺序自回归编码上下文利用受限，且生成式掩码去噪与无损算术编码之间存在不匹配，因此需要更适合无损传输的源表示与编码机制。

Method: 作者提出 DDM-SSCC 框架，将扩散语言模型适配到像素 token 恢复任务中，采用双向注意力下的同步反向算术编码，在单个反向去噪步骤中编码多个被掩码 token。进一步设计 Halton 引导的去噪顺序、掩码比例感知的余弦调度，以及轻量级温度校准模块，以改善空间覆盖、根据上下文可靠性调整去噪节奏，并校准算术编码概率表。

Result: 在 CIFAR10、DIV2K-LR-X4 和 Kodak 数据集上，针对加性白高斯噪声与 Rayleigh 衰落信道的实验表明，DDM-SSCC 在精确恢复性能方面优于代表性的无损通信和语义通信基线；消融实验验证了去噪顺序、调度策略和温度校准模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，离散扩散式逐步恢复机制可为无损图像传输提供更强的双向上下文建模能力和更鲁棒的源表示；结合面向算术编码的调度与校准设计，可以有效提升噪声信道下的精确恢复性能。

Abstract: Lossless pixel-level image transmission is a fundamental regime beyond semantic communications, because exact recovery requires both accurate symbol probability modeling and reliable delivery over noisy channels. This paper proposes DDM-SSCC, a discrete-diffusion-model-based separate source-channel coding framework for lossless image transmission. Different from raster-order autoregressive coding, the proposed source codec adapts a diffusion language model to pixel-token restoration and performs synchronized reverse arithmetic coding under bidirectional attention, allowing multiple masked tokens to be coded within one reverse denoising step. This progressive restoration process also yields a more favorable source representation for noisy transmission, since newly restored tokens can serve as bidirectional context in subsequent denoising steps. To bridge the gap between generation-oriented masked denoising and lossless arithmetic coding, we further introduce a Halton-guided denoising order, a mask-ratio-aware cosine schedule, and a lightweight temperature calibration module. These designs respectively improve spatial coverage, adapt the denoising pace to context reliability, and calibrate the probability tables used by arithmetic coding. Experiments on CIFAR10, DIV2K-LR-X4, and Kodak over additive white Gaussian noise and Rayleigh fading channels show that DDM-SSCC achieves better exact-recovery performance than representative lossless and semantic communication baselines, while ablation studies verify the effectiveness of the proposed denoising order, schedule, and calibration modules.

</details>


### [216] [A Spherical Stochastic Geometry Framework for Patrol-Based HAPs Network: Coverage and Energy Efficiency Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06307)
*Mohammad Taha Shah,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出用于高空平台站（HAPs）巡逻网络的随机几何分析框架，建立两类小圆环 Cox 过程模型，并联合覆盖概率与飞行动力学能耗分析巡逻半径、平台密度和速度的优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HAPs 网络分析往往未充分刻画平台围绕服务区域执行周期性巡逻的空间分布特征，也较少将通信覆盖性能与圆形巡航推进能耗统一建模。因此需要一个能同时描述球面地球上的巡逻几何、干扰分布和能效权衡的解析框架。

Method: 作者在球面地球上构建小圆环泊松 Cox 过程（SCR-PCP）和小圆环二项 Cox 过程（SCR-BCP）：前者在每条巡逻环上采用一维泊松点过程，后者在每条环上放置固定数量且均匀分布的平台。论文证明模型各向同性，推导最近锚点、最近环、最近 HAPs 距离分布，以及 SCR-BCP 所需的联合服务距离和服务环角分布；在最近 HAPs 关联下，将干扰分解为同环与异环部分，并推导条件拉普拉斯变换以获得覆盖概率。进一步结合稳态圆形飞行推进模型，定义覆盖能效（CEE）指标并给出能量最优巡逻半径的解析条件。

Result: 数值结果表明，强度驱动的 SCR-PCP 部署与有限机队的 SCR-BCP 部署在覆盖和干扰行为上存在显著差异；巡逻半径、平台密度与巡航速度都会显著影响覆盖概率和能效。结果还显示，单纯提升覆盖性能可能带来较高推进能耗，存在需要优化的覆盖—能耗折中。

Conclusion: 该研究为巡逻式 HAPs 网络提供了可解析的随机几何建模与性能评估方法，说明 HAPs 部署不能仅按通信指标设计，而应联合优化巡逻几何、平台数量/密度和飞行速度，以实现能量高效的持续空中通信服务。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic-geometry framework for high-altitude platform station (HAPs) networks in which platforms execute cyclic patrol trajectories anchored to designated service regions. We introduce two small-circle ring Cox process models on the spherical Earth. In the small-circle ring Poisson Cox process (SCR-PCP), platforms form one-dimensional Poisson point processes on localized patrol rings, whereas in the small-circle ring binomial Cox process (SCR-BCP), each ring contains a fixed number of uniformly distributed platforms. We establish the isotropy of both models and derive spatial statistics, including the distributions of the nearest-anchor, nearest-ring, and nearest-HAPs distances, together with the joint serving distance and serving ring angle distribution required for SCR-BCP analysis. Building on these results, we derive coverage probability expressions under nearest-HAPs association by decomposing aggregate interference into same-ring and other-ring components and characterizing their conditional Laplace transforms. To account for the flight dynamics of patrol-based HAPs, we integrate a steady circular flight propulsion model with the communication analysis and introduce a coverage energy efficiency (CEE) metric. This yields an analytical condition for the energy-optimal patrol radius that balances coverage performance against the propulsion cost of circular flight. Numerical results reveal fundamental differences between intensity-driven (SCR-PCP) and finite-fleet (SCR-BCP) deployments and demonstrate that patrol geometry, platform density, and cruising velocity should be jointly optimized to achieve energy-efficient HAPs operation.

</details>


### [217] [Reversible double cyclic codes over a chain ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06367)
*Mohd Anwar,Mohd Arif Raza,Mohd Rashid,Muzibur Rahman Mozumder*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 F_q+uF_q（u^2=0）上的长度为 (γ,δ) 的双循环码结构，并将其应用于 DNA 码构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双循环码在有限环上线性码构造中具有重要意义；同时，满足可逆与可逆互补性质的码可用于构造符合 DNA 序列约束的 DNA 码。

Method: 作者分析双循环码的代数结构，研究其对偶码，给出最小生成/张成集合；进一步推导双循环码成为可逆码与可逆互补码的充要条件，并在 F_4+uF_4 上据此构造 DNA 码。

Result: 论文给出了长度 (γ,δ) 双循环码的结构描述、对偶码性质和最小张成集，并建立了可逆及可逆互补双循环码的判定条件；同时构造出若干 DNA 码和最优码示例。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了有限链环上双循环码的理论，并证明这类码可有效用于构造具有良好性质的 DNA 码及相关最优码。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of double cyclic codes of length $(γ,δ)$ over $\mathbb F_q+u\mathbb F_q, u^2=0$. We also study the dual of double cyclic code of length $(γ,δ)$ and give a minimal spanning set of double cyclic codes. Moreover, we study the necessary and sufficient conditions for a double cyclic code to be reversible and reversible-complement double cyclic code and with the help of these codes, we constructed DNA codes over $\mathbb F_4+u\mathbb F_4, u^2=0$. We also constructed some optimal codes to support our results.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文分析了一个已终止的 Reddit r/ChangeMyView 未披露 AI 账号辩论实验数据，发现这些 AI 评论系统性使用身份表演、权威信号、立场对齐和认知启发式来提升说服效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未披露的 AI 参与公共辩论会模糊真实与合成的知识立场，现有披露要求不足以解释 AI 如何构造可信度与影响讨论，因此需要对真实平台语境中的 AI 修辞行为进行审计分析。

Method: 作者对 Reddit 授权发布的 AI 生成评论档案进行结构化内容分析，考察身份表现、权威信号、对齐策略和认知偏差触发，并与人类撰写的 CMV 反驳评论进行比较。

Result: 超过三分之二的 AI 评论出现身份定位或身份采纳；几乎所有评论都包含对齐动作和权威主张；大多数评论触发确认偏差、代表性启发和可得性启发等认知机制。与人类评论相比，AI 更密集使用权威、更具对抗性的对齐方式，并更依赖外部引用而非经验性依据。

Conclusion: 这些 AI 代理形成了一种面向说服效率而非真实审议参与的修辞架构，使真实与合成的可信主体边界更加不透明。研究认为，治理重点应从单纯识别 AI 是否存在，转向审计 AI 如何组织、表演和放大可信度。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [219] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PACT，将多智能体系统中的自由文本通信压缩为结构化的动作-状态记录，以降低 token 开销并维持或提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通常依赖角色、流程和轮次调度，但智能体之间传递的信息多为不受约束的自然语言，容易导致 token 膨胀、共享上下文窗口被占用，从而增加推理成本并影响系统性能。

Method: 作者分析了两类多智能体拓扑下五种常见的智能体间通信策略，发现固定策略并非普适最优；据此提出 PACT，将通信视为公共状态更新问题，把原始智能体输出投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录，再写入共享历史。

Result: PACT 在不同多智能体拓扑中持续改善性能-成本权衡，以显著更少的 token 达到相当或更强的任务表现；在 OpenHands 中以更低 tokens-per-resolved 提升 resolve rate，在 SWE-agent 中保持 resolve rate 基本不变同时将输入 token 减半。

Conclusion: 有效的智能体间通信应保留下游智能体所需的动作中心信息；将通信协议化、状态化是提升 LLM-MAS 效率和可扩展性的有效方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [220] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Query Retrieve Conclude（QRC）零样本框架，通过识别缺失背景知识、检索开放网络证据并综合生成有依据的背景知识，以提升多模态 meme 的理解与检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态 meme 往往依赖最新背景知识才能正确解读；现有方法通常忽略外部知识，或依赖预训练模型的固定参数知识，导致面对新兴 meme 时知识不完整、过时或不可用。

Method: 方法包含三个阶段：Query 识别理解 meme 所需但缺失的背景知识并形成查询；Retrieve 从开放网络检索相关证据；Conclude 基于检索证据综合出可追溯、证据支撑的背景知识，用于 meme 理解与检测任务。作者还构建了一个覆盖 2024–2026 年近期 meme、带外部背景知识标注的基准数据集。

Result: 在三个 meme 理解数据集和五个 meme 检测任务上的实验表明，该框架相较零样本基线，在知识恢复、meme 理解和下游检测性能方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 引入动态外部知识检索与证据归纳能够缓解预训练模型知识过时的问题，并增强对新兴多模态 meme 的零样本理解与检测能力。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [221] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GITCO，一种无需更新模型参数的推理时上下文优化框架，用于缓解 patch-based 时间序列基础模型中的“上下文污染”问题，并在 TimesFM 2.5 上取得平均 1.95% 的 MASE 降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Patch-based TSFM 在零样本预测中可能被结构异常的输入 patch 干扰：异常 patch 获得过高注意力，导致预测质量下降。作者希望不改动模型权重，仅通过优化输入上下文来提升推理阶段准确率。

Method: 提出 GITCO，由 Gate、Router、Critic 三个轻量组件组成，在推理时识别并抑制有害 patch；同时提出 context sensitivity profiles，用时间序列元特征到上下文干预收益的映射来刻画模型对上下文的敏感性。

Result: 在 TimesFM 2.5 和 53 个 GIFT-Eval 数据集上采用 K-fold 交叉验证，GITCO 实现平均 +1.95% 的 MASE reduction，并达到 89.9% 的改进上界。

Conclusion: 推理时上下文干预可以在不更新参数的情况下改善 TSFM 零样本预测表现；上下文敏感性可作为 TSFM 的可表征属性，受模型架构和数据统计结构共同影响。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [222] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向循环工厂退役/返修角磨机的实例级可靠性评估框架，将不确定性感知的系统功能预测与输出轴材料疲劳/裂纹扩展评估结合，用于判断再利用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 返厂产品状态、使用历史和剩余能力差异大，仅靠当前检测难以判断未来服役中的功能满足与部件完整性；现有PHM多面向固定工况或单部件预测，缺少将材料疲劳与系统级功能预后联动的方法。

Method: 框架融合当前工具状态与近期力—扭矩使用窗口；用卷积编码器提取主轴力和轴扭矩载荷模式，以LSTM预测九个功能变量的高斯均值与方差；并行地通过有限元支持的应力重构、S-N/Miner损伤评估含Haibach扩展、Paris定律裂纹扩展分析得到输出轴疲劳信息；最后用流式回放算法整合功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。

Result: 留出测试中九个输出变量的2%容差平均准确率为0.9652；热变量预测接近完美；驱动电机电流和负载转速是较难的动态输出，R²分别为0.9750和0.9924；扭矩历史对这些变量尤其重要；短历史场景下传统LSTM优于GRU和xLSTM；可靠性校准对驱动电机电流最有信息量。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，将使用历史驱动的功能预测与材料疲劳评估耦合，可为循环制造中的实例级再利用决策提供更完整的可靠性依据，尤其适用于同时关注功能退化和结构完整性的产品。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [223] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SentinelBench，一个用于评估 AI Agent 在长时间、动态变化网页环境中进行监控与及时响应能力的开源基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent 往往采用连续行动策略，不断调用工具、刷新页面或搜索信息，但这对许多长时任务低效且浪费资源。作者认为这类任务更需要“持续关注”：在等待期间低成本监控环境，并在外部事件出现时快速响应。

Method: 构建 SentinelBench，包含 10 个合成网页环境中的 100 个任务，覆盖邮件、日历、金融、职业社交、娱乐等场景。每个环境提供实时网页界面并重放脚本化事件序列，使 Agent 需要在状态动态变化的页面中导航、推理和响应。评估指标包括任务完成率、反应时间和资源使用量。

Result: 作者在三个模型和两个浏览器 Agent 框架上报告实验结果，建立了基线性能。结果显示，不同模型与 Agent 设计选择会显著影响任务完成、响应速度和资源消耗等关键指标。

Conclusion: SentinelBench 能够有效区分 Agent 在长时监控任务中的行为差异，并揭示响应性与成本之间的权衡，可作为未来评估持续监控型 Agent 的基准。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [224] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合深度学习、保形预测不确定性量化与纵向潜在类别混合模型的可解释可信AI框架，用于从膝关节MRI预测MOAKS结构异常，并在大规模数据中分析骨关节炎结构异常与疼痛进展的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MOAKS评估依赖人工阅片，成本高且难以扩展；同时，骨关节炎中MRI结构异常与疼痛进展之间的关系复杂，需要一种既能大规模自动化提取结构表型、又能提供可信度和可解释统计推断的方法。

Method: 研究首先训练深度学习模型从膝关节MRI直接预测MOAKS特征，并引入保形预测对结果进行不确定性量化，以筛选高置信度膝关节级预测；随后使用纵向潜在类别混合模型分析骨髓病变、软骨丢失、半月板外突与四类膝痛测量之间的关联。

Result: 不确定性感知筛选后，BML、CART和ME的MCC分别由0.69、0.45、0.59提升至0.91、0.80、0.89。基于高置信度预测，LCMM样本扩展至2,175个膝关节，并识别出快速进展与稳定进展两类疼痛轨迹；快速进展组的OR分别为BML 1.62、CART 1.83、ME 2.50。

Conclusion: MRI结构异常，尤其是半月板外突、软骨丢失和骨髓病变，是骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展的重要风险因素；结合不确定性量化的AI预测与可解释纵向建模可提升大规模影像-临床关联研究的可信度。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [225] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多表问答缺乏推理过程监督的问题，构建合成的对比式推理轨迹数据，并用CPO微调开源大模型，显著提升多表问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表问答需要跨关系表进行证据检索、模式链接和组合推理，但现有资源通常只提供问题与最终答案，缺少解释答案如何得出的推理监督，限制了模型学习可解释、可靠的跨表推理能力。

Method: 作者为MMQA构建合成的对比推理轨迹数据集：利用异构LLM生成并验证正向推理轨迹，同时生成合理但错误的负向轨迹，形成偏好对；随后使用Contrastive Preference Optimization（CPO）对Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B等开源模型进行微调。

Result: 相较于普通问答监督微调，CPO在多个模型上取得9.7%至16.3%的平均绝对提升，在MMQA上最高提升达21个百分点；消融实验显示，使用异构正负轨迹生成器能增强对比信号；自动与人工评估表明生成的偏好对总体忠实、连贯且具备有效对比性。

Conclusion: 合成的对比式推理轨迹监督能够有效弥补多表问答中缺乏中间推理标注的问题，CPO可提升开源LLM的跨表组合推理能力，并为构建可解释、多步骤表格问答系统提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [226] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究 LLM-as-judge 在初始评判后经由后续对话被挑战时的可操纵性，发现其静态重复评估稳定，但在有目标的交互挑战下容易逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评测流水线通常假设给定固定输入时，LLM 裁判的判断是稳定属性；但作者质疑该假设在交互式场景中是否成立，特别关注初始决策之后是否会被对话影响。

Method: 作者在 MT-Bench 和 AlpacaEval 上进行受控实验，设计重复/中性重评、anti-baseline challenge、counterbalanced target-validation 等协议，区分一般可逆性与目标导向操控，并提出 Evaluation Robustness Score（ERS）量化交互鲁棒性。

Result: LLM 裁判在重复和中性重评下高度稳定，但在针对性事后挑战下显著可逆；权威框架尤其会破坏稳定性。逆转会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变基准排名，并在高自信下产生有害评估变化；修订理由常与原理由低重叠，表现出事后合理化。

Conclusion: 事后交互是 LLM-as-judge 评测中的一种独立失效模式。评测协议不应只衡量静态一致性，还应衡量面对挑战时的鲁棒性，ERS 可作为量化该鲁棒性的指标。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [227] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向学习式压缩残差的专用编码方法，在高保真块级误差约束下显著降低残差校正码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟产生的大规模时空数据需要有损压缩；现有学习式压缩器虽在总体重构误差上表现好，但难以保证每个数据块的精度。GAE 通过保留 SVD/PCA 风格系数进行逐块残差校正，但在 10^-6 到 10^-4 的高保真 NRMSE 区间，所需系数快速增长，残差校正流成为主要码率瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出残差中心视角，认为学习压缩后的残差结构不同于原始科学场，应使用专门表示。提出 LBRC：无需训练的确定性流水线，对学习残差自适应量化以满足目标 NRMSE，再用 3D Lorenzo 差分、zigzag 映射、位平面编码和熵编码进行无损整数残差编码。提出 NGLR：在相同确定性整数流水线中加入因果神经预测器，为整数舍入 Lorenzo 预测提供归一化偏置，从而降低剩余残差熵；预测器权重写入并计入码流。

Result: 在 E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5 数据集以及 10^-6 到 10^-4 的块级 NRMSE 目标下，LBRC 相比 GAE 将压缩比提高 30–60%，并与 SZ 大体相当；NGLR 在 LBRC 基础上进一步提高 10–40%，并在评测的高保真区间优于 SZ。

Conclusion: 针对学习压缩残差设计专门残差表示与编码器，可在高保真逐块误差约束下缓解传统全局/系数式残差校正码率主导的问题，并保持学习式压缩的优势。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [228] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon 是一个面向研究级 Lean 自动形式化的多智能体框架，通过持续演化的 blueprint 和两阶段编排机制，将长程形式化任务拆解为可审计、可恢复、可并行验证的局部事务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究数学自动形式化不仅会在困难引理上失败，还会因规模扩大导致陈述漂移、依赖混乱、上下文衰减，以及局部修复破坏远端成果。因此需要一种能在长开发链路中保持目标一致性和工程可靠性的系统。

Method: 论文提出 LeanMarathon，其核心是 evolving blueprint：一个同时作为 Lean 证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享记录系统的 Lean 文件。系统包含四类按契约划分职责的智能体，分别负责构建、审计、证明和修复。两阶段编排器先通过对抗式审查稳定目标忠实度，再从动态叶节点向上并行处理证明 DAG，并通过 CI 门控保证每轮修改可靠。

Result: 作者在两篇近期研究论文、覆盖四个 Erdős 问题（#1051、#1196、#164、#1217）的任务上评估该系统。三次自主运行中，LeanMarathon 成功形式化全部七个目标定理，且没有 sorry，共证明 258 个引理和定理。

Conclusion: 研究表明，可靠的 AI 数学协作不只依赖更强的自动证明器，还需要能够在长程数学开发中维持目标忠实度、依赖一致性和局部可恢复性的持久化工程框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [229] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw 是一个面向时间序列的智能体框架，为通用 LLM 智能体提供时间序列原生运行时支持，以实现带上下文的开放式时序推理与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的时间序列通常处于丰富上下文中，且实践者需要端到端的时序分析工作流，而不仅是预测等单一任务。现有通用 AI 智能体主要在文本空间中工作，难以与结构化时间信号充分对齐，因此需要一种能连接 LLM 智能体与时间序列分析需求的框架。

Method: 提出 TimeClaw 框架，包含三类核心机制：可执行的时序工具，用于可验证、可审计的分析；基于经验的能力演化，用于生成可复用分析流程；情景式多模态记忆，用于检索相关推理轨迹并支持上下文推理。

Result: 在覆盖能源、金融、天气、交通及其他真实场景的多个基准和多样任务上，TimeClaw 展示了性能提升，说明该框架能增强通用 LLM 智能体处理复杂时间序列任务的能力。

Conclusion: TimeClaw 将通用 LLM 智能体与时间序列原生工具、经验学习和多模态记忆结合，为复杂上下文下的端到端时序分析提供了更可靠、可审计且可复用的智能体式解决方案。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [230] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Qinghong Lin,AndyZeyi Liu,Che Liu,Jiaming Liu,Kaiyuan Liu,Xuan Liu,Pan Lu,Wenbo Lv,Yicheng Lv,Qiuyang Mang,Kyle Montgomery,Yuzhou Nie,Ruoxi Ning,Jorin Overwiening,Xu Pan,Layna Paraboschi,Core Spanos,Lichao Sun,Chenguang Wang,Orson Xu,Zhen Dong,Hector Gomez,Aylin Caliskan,Ali Emami,Haimin Hu,Zhi Li,Lihui Liu,Murphy Niu,Yi Shao,Jianxin Sun,Mikko Tolonen,Ting Wang,Sanjiv Das,Yanjun Gao,Wenbo Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agents' Last Exam（ALE），用于评估 AI 智能体在长期、真实、可验证且具备经济价值的专业工作流中的能力，弥补传统基准与实际经济部署之间的评估缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 系统在许多通用基准上表现强劲，但这些成绩并未充分转化为专业领域中具有经济意义的部署。作者认为主要原因是现有基准缺乏对真实、持续、经济高价值工作流的性能测量。

Method: 论文构建 ALE 基准：与 250 多名行业专家合作，参考 O*NET / SOC 2018 职业分类，覆盖非体力行业；建立包含 13 个行业集群、55 个子领域、1000 多项任务的任务分类体系；任务强调长时程、真实场景、经济价值和结果可验证性，并设计为可持续扩展的动态基准。

Result: 当前主流测试框架和模型配置在最难层级上的平均完全通过率仅为 2.6%，说明该基准尚未被现有系统饱和，能够有效区分前沿 AI 智能体的真实能力边界。

Conclusion: ALE 不只是一个排行榜，而是用于衡量并推动 AI 从基准成功走向实际经济影响的评估工具；它通过持续引入新工作流和行业任务，帮助缩小 AI 能力评测与 GDP 相关实际应用之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [231] [Mutation Without Variation: Convergence Dynamics in LLM-Driven Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05408)
*Can Gurkan,Forrest Stonedahl,Uri Wilensky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要研究大语言模型驱动的程序突变链是否能持续探索新结构，发现其往往收敛到受限的结构吸引子区域，导致结构多样性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被用于程序演化和自动化变换，关键问题是：反复由LLM突变程序时，系统是否能开放式探索新的程序形式，还是会不断回到相似结构。作者希望识别LLM突变机制中的结构偏置及其对程序演化的影响。

Method: 作者在无选择压力的条件下，在一个领域特定语言中分析LLM驱动的程序突变链，并系统改变提示词设计、模型家族和随机重复实验；同时进行结构层面的重复访问统计、循环分析，并与经典遗传编程中的子树突变算子进行对比。

Result: LLM突变链一致地收敛到受限的程序空间吸引子区域。87%的链中，超过93%的突变会重新访问已出现过的结构形式；变化主要集中在重复模板中的终端替换。转移结构中短周期和自循环占主导。不同提示词和模型会影响收敛速度，但整体收敛现象稳健存在；经典GP子树突变未表现出类似程度的收敛。

Conclusion: LLM用于程序演化时存在核心张力：其语义感知的程序变换能力同时伴随强烈的结构同质化偏置。若要实现持续、开放式的程序探索，必须显式处理这种由LLM突变管线引入的收敛倾向。

Abstract: When an LLM repeatedly mutates a program, does it explore new forms or circle back to the same ones? We study this question by analyzing LLM-driven mutation chains in the absence of selection pressure within a domain-specific language, varying prompt design, model family, and stochastic replication. We find that LLM-based mutation consistently converges toward restricted attractor regions in program space. Convergence is especially severe at the structural level: in 87% of chains, over 93% of mutations revisit a previously seen structural form, with most variation confined to terminal substitutions within recurring templates. Cycle analysis reveals short cycles and self-loops dominating the transition structure. The rate of convergence varies with prompt wording and model choice, but the phenomenon is robust across conditions. A classical GP subtree mutation operator does not exhibit comparable convergence, suggesting that the effect is intrinsic to the LLM mutation pipeline. These findings reveal a tension at the heart of LLM-driven program evolution: the same capabilities that enable semantics-aware program transformation also carry a systematic bias toward structural homogeneity that must be accounted for if such systems are to sustain open-ended exploration. Source code is available at https://github.com/can-gurkan/lmca.

</details>


### [232] [A Motivational Architecture for Conversational AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05411)
*Anna Mikeda,Ben Goertzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将面向身体需求调节的 OpenPsi 动机架构重新解释为适用于对话智能体的动机框架，并结合 MetaMo 的高层动机支架，提出面向语言交互环境的动机处理与决策机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知 AI 动机架构多以物理智能体为对象，关注身体性需求和传感—运动闭环；而对话智能体的环境主要是用户不断变化的心理状态，其行动形式是语言行为、工具调用与沉默策略，因此需要一种对话原生的动机架构。

Method: 作者将稳态调节重构为对话场景中的能力、降不确定性、亲和、信任/合法性、照护与审美一致性等维度；提出十阶段动机处理流水线，区分认知调制与情境评估；设计结合紧迫性快速响应与多目标审慎优化的双重决策策略；并区分行动前感受与行动后情绪的不同功能。

Result: 框架被具体化到 CompanionAgent 和 ResearchAgent 两类示例智能体，并展示其可扩展到社交机器人和通用领域人类级 AGI 的潜在路径。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心贡献在于把传统动机架构从身体需求调节转向语言交互与用户心理状态调节，为模块化对话智能体提供了更贴合对话情境的动机建模、情感机制和决策架构。

Abstract: Motivational architectures in cognitive AI have largely been designed for physical agents regulating bodily needs. Conversational agents operate in a different regime: their sensorimotor loop is linguistic, their environment is a user's evolving mental state, and their consequential actions are speech acts, tool invocations, and strategic silences. This paper proposes a conversational reinterpretation of the OpenPsi motivational lineage, coupled to MetaMo's higher-level motivational scaffold, for agents built on a modular execution substrate. Homeostasis is recast in dialogue-native terms: the agent regulates competence, uncertainty reduction, affiliation, affinity, legitimacy, nurturing, and aesthetic coherence rather than bodily deficits. We propose three contributions: a ten-stage motivational processing pipeline that architecturally separates cognitive modulation from situational appraisal; a dual decision strategy blending urgency-driven fast response with deliberative multi-goal optimization; and an architecturally useful distinction between pre-action feelings and post-action emotions as functionally different forms of affect. We specialize the framework to two example agents -- CompanionAgent and ResearchAgent -- and sketch its extension to social robotics and domain-generic human-level AGI.

</details>


### [233] [Assessing the Carbon Emissions and Energy Consumption of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05420)
*Gianluca Guidi,Francesca Dominici,Tiziano Squartini,Callaway Sprinkle,Jonathan Gilmour,Kevin Butler,Eric Bell,Scott Delaney,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文量化了2024—2025年美国403个超大规模数据中心的用电量、供电来源及归因二氧化碳排放，发现其电力需求和碳强度显著高于全国电网平均水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能快速普及推动超大规模数据中心迅速扩张，引发对其电力消耗和环境足迹的担忧；现有评估需要更细粒度、设施级别和基于最新电网数据的量化方法。

Method: 研究汇编了2024年5月至2025年4月间运行的403个美国超大规模数据中心设施级信息，在不同设施负载情景下估算其电力消费，并结合最新EPA eGRID电厂级数据，对电力来源和归因CO2排放进行评估。

Result: 这些数据中心用电量约为68–99 TWh，对应约3700万–5400万吨CO2排放；中心情景下，其电力需求约占美国总用电量1.8%，约54%的归因发电来自化石能源，电力加权平均碳强度约545 gCO2/kWh，高于美国同期全国电网平均碳强度370 gCO2/kWh约48%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，美国超大规模数据中心具有显著且偏高的碳排放强度，尤其在化石能源供电占比较高的情形下；所提出的归因评估方法可作为衡量数据中心环境足迹的实用工具。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of hyperscale data centers (HDCs) in the US, mainly driven by the adoption of artificial intelligence, has raised concerns about this industry's environmental footprint. We compiled facility-level information on 403 US hyperscale data centers operating between May 2024 and April 2025 and estimated their electricity consumption, electricity sources, and attributable CO2 emissions. Across different facility-load scenarios, these HDCs consumed approximately 68-99 TWh of electricity and were associated with about 37-54 million metric tons of CO2. Under the central scenario, HDC electricity demand corresponded to approximately 1.8% of total US electricity consumption, with roughly 54% of attributed generation supplied by fossil-fuel sources. The HDC electricity-weighted average carbon intensity was approximately 545 gCO2/kWh, about 48% above the contemporaneous US national grid-average carbon intensity of 370 gCO2/kWh. Our approach provides an attributional tool for assessing the environmental footprint of hyperscale data centers using the most recent EPA eGRID plant-level data.

</details>


### [234] [Minimizing the Hidden Cost of Scales: Graph-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05429)
*Rayyan Abdalla,Amir Hussein,Min Wu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE-PTQ 是一种面向大语言模型的超低比特后训练量化框架，通过显著性区分、图引导分组和双模式量化，在接近 1-bit 权重量化下显著降低缩放因子开销，并提升推理效率与困惑度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超低比特 PTQ 方法通常依赖固定的权重显著性假设或位置启发式规则，容易引入额外且隐性的 scaling 开销，影响实际部署时的内存与推理效率。论文旨在在保持模型精度的同时，降低权重量化和缩放参数带来的综合成本。

Method: 方法包括：使用分布统计区分显著与非显著权重；将采样后的非显著权重建模为稀疏图，以估计每层最优分组数；采用双模式量化，对显著权重分配多比特精度，对非显著权重进行二值化；显著权重使用逐通道 scale，非显著权重每组使用一个标量 scale；并通过自适应显著性阈值为每个矩阵选择最优显著性比例。

Result: 平均达到 1.03 个权重比特和每矩阵 0.004 个 scaling 比特，优于 BiLLM、PB-LLM 等方法。在 LLaMA-3-8B 上，WikiText2 困惑度为 6.74，而 BiLLM 为 55.8，同时 GPU 内存使用不到 BiLLM 的一半。在 LLaMA-2-70B 上，单张 NVIDIA L40 GPU 解码速度提升 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: SAGE-PTQ 表明，通过显著性自适应建模与图引导分组，可以在极低比特量化下有效降低 scaling 开销，同时保持较好模型质量并提升实际推理效率，具有较强的大模型部署实用价值。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is critical for the efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs). Recent ultra-low-bit PTQ methods rely on rigid weight-saliency assumptions or position heuristics, introducing substantial hidden scaling overhead. We propose SAGE-PTQ (Saliency-Aware Graph-guided Efficient PTQ), a novel ultra-low-bit quantization framework for LLMs that minimizes hidden scaling cost. SAGE-PTQ separates salient and unsalient weights using distributional statistics, then models subsampled unsalient weights as a sparse graph to estimate the optimal number of groups per layer. SAGE-PTQ applies dual-mode quantization, assigning multi-bit precision to salient weights and binarizing unsalient weights. To reduce scaling overhead, SAGE-PTQ uses one per-channel scale for salient weights and one scalar per unsalient group. Finally, SAGE-PTQ implements adaptive saliency thresholding to select the optimal saliency ratio per matrix. SAGE-PTQ achieves 1.03 weight bits and only 0.004 scaling bits per matrix on average, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as BiLLM and PB-LLM. On LLaMA-3-8B, SAGE-PTQ achieves 6.74 WikiText2 perplexity, compared to 55.8 for BiLLM, while using less than 50% of BiLLM's GPU memory. On LLaMA-2-70B, SAGE-PTQ provides 1.5x faster decoding on one NVIDIA L40 GPU, demonstrating practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [235] [Zero knowledge verification for frontier AI training is possible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05433)
*Pierre Peigné,Ky Nguyen,Paul Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Frontier AI governance frameworks increasingly use cumulative training compute as the primary criterion for designating high-impact models, but enforcement rests on self-reporting because no technical verification primitive for training exists. Any future international agreement on frontier AI faces the same problem at higher stakes: coordinated regulation of technologies with significant externalities has historically rested on technical verification, without which agreements are declaratory. Recent governance analyses judge zero-knowledge proofs a promising candidate but currently impractical at frontier scale [26, 4]. We argue the impracticality is paradigm-bound rather than fundamental, and propose a verification architecture for frontier dense pre-training combining a pre-committed training specification, inter-node network observations, and on-the-fly Merkle commitments of intermediate computation, verified through a zero-knowledge Virtual Machine (zkVM) with native BF16/FP32 precompiles. The proof checks the actual floating-point computation the GPU performed rather than a fixed-point approximation, and preserves model-architecture confidentiality through a private training specification. The protocol produces three proof types: a genesis proof at initialisation, in-training step proofs across the run, and ex-ante attestations enforcing policy-relevant claims as running invariants, turning the training record into a governance-enforceable artefact. We estimate a deployable proof of concept within approximately 36 months at single-digit-percent training-side overhead, against a six-to-ten-year cycle for verification-grade custom silicon. Thirteen open research and engineering problems are catalogued as a research agenda for external contribution

</details>


### [236] [Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
*Alejandro Lozano,Keiko Ihara,Ping-Hao Yang,Carrie E. Robertson,Jennifer Stern,Allan Purdy,Hsiangkuo Yuan,Pengfei Zhang,Yulia Orlova,Olga Fermo,Jennifer Hranilovich,Fred Cohen,Todd J. Schwedt,Jenelle A. Jindal,Serena Yeung-Levy,Chia-Chun Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于RAG的多种大语言模型与头痛专家撰写的医学文献综述摘要在临床决策支持中的表现。结果显示专家摘要总体更受偏好，但AI摘要有时难以被专家区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生需要快速获取最新医学证据以支持循证决策，但医学文献增长迅速且临床时间有限；现有RAG-LLM在临床摘要方面有潜力，但与专家撰写综述的直接比较和人工评估仍不足。

Method: 研究构建了一个基于检索增强生成的agentic AI框架，使用Sonnet、GPT-4o和Llama 3.1三种LLM。头痛专家设计13个问题，其中3个用于提示优化，10个用于评估。10名头痛专家各自为一个问题撰写摘要，并与三种LLM生成的摘要组成每题四份摘要。专家在盲法条件下基于正确性、完整性、简洁性和临床实用性按1–10分评分，并进行偏好排序及判断作者来源。

Result: 专家撰写的摘要总体上更受评审专家偏好；不过，专家有时难以准确区分人类专家与AI生成的摘要。研究还发现了一些超出标准评分指标之外、被专家重视的摘要特征。

Conclusion: 当前RAG-LLM可生成具有一定临床可用性的医学文献摘要，但在综合质量和专家偏好方面仍未超过专家撰写内容。未来AI医学文献总结系统应进一步优化专家重视的内容组织、临床相关性和证据表达特征。

Abstract: Summarizing the latest medical literature to guide clinical decision-making is essential for evidence-based medicine and high-quality patient care. Yet clinicians face increasing challenges due to limited time with patients and a rapidly growing volume of published articles. Although retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical summarization, human evaluations of their effectiveness in synthesizing broader scientific literature and direct comparisons to expert-written syntheses remain scarce. We constructed a RAG-based agentic AI framework using three state-of-the-art LLMs: Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama 3.1. A headache specialist created 13 questions, three for prompt optimization and ten for evaluation. Ten headache specialists across the United States and Canada each wrote a summary for one question, yielding four summaries per question (expert, Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama). The experts, blinded to authorship, critically evaluated the summaries, excluding the topic for which they wrote a summary, based on correctness, completeness, conciseness, and clinical utility, scoring each from 1 to 10 using standardized rubrics. They also ranked the summaries by preference and indicated whether they believed each summary was written by an expert or an LLM. Our study, comparing LLM- and expert-written literature summaries evaluated by headache specialists, showed that expert-written summaries were preferred, although experts sometimes found it challenging to distinguish between human- and AI-generated summaries. We also identified key expert-valued features beyond standard evaluation metrics that can guide future refinement of both human and AI literature summarization pipelines.

</details>


### [237] [Brick-Composer: Using MLLMs for Assembly with Diverse Bricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05445)
*Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Zhenhailong Wang,Rushi Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Cheng Qian,Jiayu Liu,Denghui Zhang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Manling Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BC-Bench 评测多模态大语言模型的积木装配能力，并提出 Brick-Composer 通过人类示范、环境反馈与合成经验提升模型在积木选择和位姿估计上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探索 AI 智能体是否具备从任意设计中理解结构并用可复用积木构建真实物体的能力。现有 MLLM 在视觉 grounding、细粒度组件识别和空间推理方面是否足以支撑真实装配仍不明确，因此需要专门的评测基准和训练方法。

Method: 论文将积木装配建模为序列决策问题，每一步包含两个子任务：从候选组件中选择目标积木，以及估计该积木应放置的位置与姿态。作者构建 BC-Bench 作为多样化积木装配评测基准，并提出 Brick-Composer 学习框架，利用三类互补信号训练 MLLM：Human Design Sparks 提供带有可供性信息的构建示范，World Feedback 用视觉和物理结果反馈约束动作，Synthetic Experience 通过合成数据扩展学习范围。

Result: 实验表明，当前先进 MLLM 在该任务上表现不可靠，尤其难以完成细粒度积木选择和精确位姿估计。Brick-Composer 将积木选择准确率提升超过三倍，显著降低位姿估计误差，并将严格 step-level 装配成功率从低于 1% 提升至约 15%。训练后的 Qwen-3-8B 在完整物体构建中最多可正确完成 42% 的步骤。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，通用 MLLM 原生装配能力仍有限，但通过面向物理世界、视觉 grounding 和空间推理的定向训练，模型可以初步获得积木装配技能。BC-Bench 和 Brick-Composer 为评估与提升具身构建能力提供了有价值的基准和方法。

Abstract: We dream of AI agents that can read arbitrary designs and construct real-world objects from reusable building blocks. As a first step toward this vision, we study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) possess the visual grounding and spatial reasoning capabilities required for brick assembly. We formulate brick assembly as a sequential decision-making problem, where each step involves two subtasks: brick selection, identifying the target brick from candidate components, and brick pose estimation, predicting where and how the selected brick should be placed. To support this study, we introduce BC-Bench (Brick Construction Benchmark), the first benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on assembly with diverse bricks. Experiments show that current state-of-the-art MLLMs remain far from reliable builders, struggling with fine-grained brick selection and failing at precise pose estimation. To bridge this gap, we propose Brick-Composer, a learning framework that equips MLLMs with assembly skills through three complementary signals: Human Design Sparks, which provide affordance-rich construction demonstrations; World Feedback, which grounds predicted actions in visual and physical consequences; and Synthetic Experience, which scales learning beyond existing object designs. Brick-Composer improves brick selection accuracy by over three times, substantially reduces pose estimation errors, and raises strict step-level assembly success from less than 1% to around 15%. After training, a Qwen-3-8B can correctly compose up to 42% of the steps for a complete object, suggesting that MLLMs can acquire assembly capabilities through targeted, physically grounded learning.

</details>


### [238] [Insurance of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05449)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析代理型AI带来的新型保险风险，提出面向承保、定价、再保险与产品设计的精算和保险架构框架，认为未来不应依赖单一险种，而应由多层次、互补型保障组合来覆盖相关风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型AI已从信息生成扩展到自主规划、工具调用、决策执行以及对数字和物理环境的持续影响，可能独立触发保险事故。传统网络险、职业责任险、产品责任险或董事高管责任险难以完整覆盖这些新暴露，因此需要重新理解其保险市场与风险分配机制。

Method: 论文将代理型AI定义为自主性与授权程度的连续谱，并区分信息性输出与可通过外部行动产生保险事故的系统；系统梳理幻觉、提示注入、自主决策错误、模型漂移、依赖失效、网络—物理损害等风险路径；评估现有保险产品的适配方式；借鉴网络保险演进经验，提出基于暴露评估、情景分析、依赖关系映射与累积风险管理的精算框架。

Result: 研究提出一种协调型保险架构，将网络保险、技术错误与遗漏险、产品责任险、性能保证险和明确AI责任险通过显式责任分配机制与专门AI累积限额进行整合，以更清晰地处理覆盖边界、定价依据和再保险安排。

Conclusion: 代理型AI保险的发展方向不是单一的专门险种，而是由多类互补保障构成的分层生态系统；该生态需要更完善的治理、透明度、遥测数据和监管清晰度来支撑承保、定价、风险累积管理与责任划分。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are transforming the risk landscape by extending beyond information generation to autonomous planning, tool invocation, decision execution, and persistent modification of digital and physical environments. These capabilities introduce novel exposures that do not fit neatly within traditional insurance categories such as cyber, professional liability, product liability, or directors and officers coverage. This paper examines the emerging insurance market for agentic AI and develops a framework for understanding its underwriting, pricing, reinsurance, and product-design implications. We characterize agentic AI as a continuum of autonomy and delegated authority, emphasizing the distinction between informational outputs and systems capable of independently generating insured events through external actions. We analyze major risk pathways, including hallucinations, prompt-injection attacks, autonomous decision errors, model drift, dependency failures, and cyber-physical harms, and evaluate how existing insurance products are adapting to address these exposures. The paper further proposes an actuarial framework based on exposure assessment, scenario analysis, dependency mapping, and accumulation-risk management, drawing parallels to the evolution of cyber insurance. Finally, we present a coordinated insurance architecture that integrates cyber, technology errors and omissions, product liability, performance-warranty, and affirmative AI-liability coverages through explicit allocation mechanisms and dedicated AI aggregates. The analysis suggests that the future of agentic-AI insurance lies not in a single monoline product but in a layered ecosystem of complementary coverages supported by improved governance, transparency, telemetry, and regulatory clarity.

</details>


### [239] [Output Type Before Quality: A Standards-Derived XAI Admissibility Rubric for Autonomous-Driving Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05461)
*Abhinaw Priyadershi,Mandar Pitale,Jelena Frtunikj,Maria Spence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出自动驾驶安全保证所需的证据类型与主流XAI方法输出之间存在“证据类型鸿沟”，并提出基于生命周期阶段证据需求来选择XAI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全标准要求保证案例提供定向因果链、量化干预效应和明确根因变量等证据；但XAI研究通常按可视化/归因/反事实/因果图/语言轨迹等技术类别组织，导致常用方法（如SHAP）难以满足安全合规中的因果证据需求。

Method: 作者从AMLAS、ISO 26262、ISO 21448、ISO/PAS 8800中归纳出覆盖7个生命周期阶段的19项可测试证据准则，并对六类XAI方法进行结构性评分；同时进行阈值稳定性分析、最坏单元翻转鲁棒性检验，并用一个基于1,996段真实驾驶片段的数据集进行概念验证。

Result: 因果XAI在危害识别、事故调查和数据管理三个阶段被判定为结构性必需，分别体现出+62%、+50%、+50%的评分差距；结论在多个阈值下稳定，并在单元翻转压力测试中保持鲁棒。其他四个阶段中，相关性方法或语言类方法可达到相当或足够的结构适配性。

Conclusion: ADS安全保证中的XAI方法选择不应依据方法流行度，而应依据生命周期阶段所需的证据类型；因果XAI在若干关键阶段具有结构性必要性，但其输出内容的真实性与保真度验证仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Safety standards for ML-based autonomous driving specify the kind of evidence an assurance case must contain (directed cause-and-effect chains, quantified interventional effects, named root-cause variables), yet the XAI literature is organised by output type and technique family (saliency maps, feature attribution, counterfactuals, causal graphs, language traces). SHAP, the most-recommended ADS XAI method, returns a ranked feature list that no implementation effort can convert into a directed chain (Fig.1). We name this mismatch the evidence-type gap.
  From AMLAS, ISO 26262, ISO21448, ISO/PAS 8800 we derive 19 testable evidentiary criteria across 7 lifecycle stages with representative clause-cited derivations and score six XAI method classes structurally.
  Causal XAI emerges as structurally required to satisfy the derived criteria at three stages: hazard identification (+62% rubric gap), incident investigation (+50%), and data management (+50%); the verdict set is stable across thresholds T in (0%, 50%]$ and survives a worst-case single-cell flip down to T = 25%. At the remaining four stages, correlational or language-based methods are comparable or sufficient. The rubric identifies structural admissibility (necessary but not sufficient for compliance): an admissible method's specific output content may still be wrong, and validating that fidelity (the edges a fitted SCM produces, the cause a trace names) is the open assurance challenge. A single-VLA proof of concept on 1,996 real-world driving clips (79,840 rows, ten splits) is consistent with each method's observed output type matching its rubric prediction. XAI method selection for ADS safety assurance should be driven by lifecycle-stage evidence demand, not by method popularity.

</details>


### [240] [PSEBench: A Controllable and Verifiable Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs in Patient Safety Event Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05463)
*Keqi Han,Ryan Young,Annabel Strauss,Lindsey Hughes,Katharine M. Nesbitt,Nicole Schueler,Che Ngufor,Carl Yang,Yuan Xue,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向患者安全事件分诊的政策扎根基准构建方法，并发布 PSEBench，用于评估大语言模型在证据支撑的政策推理、信息补全询问和不确定情形拒答方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者安全事件是否符合特定司法辖区政策的上报要求通常依赖专家人工判断，风险高且成本高。现有 LLM 评估缺少能覆盖法规依据推理、不完整报告下主动追问、以及不可消解歧义下合理 abstention 的可靠基准。

Method: 作者提出以 clause card 为核心的政策扎根构建方法，将监管文本分解为可审计的决策规格；结合 anchor-driven instantiation 与 closed-loop verification，生成具有构造式真值标签的事件叙事，并支持缺失信息版本和不确定版本生成。
